Returns `{newWeights, oldWeights, gradient, reason: "applied" | "learning-paused" | "insufficient-data" | "no-defaults", applied, sampleCount?, stepSize?, have?, need?}`.

**Apply-pipeline integration.** `apply-pipeline.js` now records the "before" measurement after every successful apply: thread `opts.snapshot` through `panel.js → AesStrategy.apply` so the same snapshot the modal already composed is reused (one fewer round trip); falls back to `AesStrategy.snapshot({})` when `opts.snapshot` is missing. Recording is gated on `learningEnabled === true && !aborted && okCount > 0` — fully-skipped applies don't pollute the ring. The audit envelope's `weights` field captures whatever `AesStrategyLearn.getCurrentWeights()` returns at apply time so future learn cycles can attribute the observed deltas to that exact weight set.

**Strategy modal — Learning section.** Mounted as the fifth body region (after summary chips, settings strip, decisions list, per-aircraft accordion). Renders: (1) three count chips — Recorded · Ready · Attributed; (2) controls row — `Learning enabled` checkbox (pause toggle, persists `settings.learningEnabled`) + step-size range input 0..0.20 step 0.01 with live readout (persists `settings.learningStepSize`); (3) action row — `Capture pending outcomes` (calls `tryCaptureAfter` against the modal's current snapshot), `Run learn cycle` (calls `learn({force: true})` so the user can manually drive a cycle even when learning is paused — paused gates auto-recording, not manual triggers), `Reset weights` (window.confirm + `resetWeights("user-reset")`); (4) current-weights grid — every weight key with its current value to 3 dp; (5) recent-history list — last 5 entries with timestamp + reason + sample count + step size. Every action toasts the outcome and re-renders the section in place.

**Dashboard tile — Learning card.** Adds a "Learning" card below the last-apply card showing one summary line ("3 outcomes · 1 attributed · 2 ready · enabled · step 0.05") and a tail line for the most recent weight change. Watches `aesStrategy:learn:outcomes` and `aesStrategy:learn:weights:current` storage keys so the tile re-renders after every `record()` / learn cycle without the user reopening it.

**Learnt weights threading.** Both `panel.js` (modal compose) and `strategy-tile.js` (inline preview + tile-body compose) now call `AesStrategy.scoreRoutes(snapshot, await AesStrategyLearn.getCurrentWeights())` instead of the bare `scoreRoutes(snapshot)` — so a learnt weight set immediately changes the proposed plan ranking on the next compose without a page reload. Both call sites graceful-fallback to defaults when the learn module isn't loaded (so dashboard pages without the learn manifest entries still work).

**Settings tie-in.** `learningEnabled` and `learningStepSize` were pre-staged in Slice 4's `AesStrategySettings` defaults — Slice 5 wires the modal controls + apply-pipeline gate. No new settings keys; the schema is stable.

**Manifest order.** Outcomes + learn registered before apply-pipeline + panel + strategy-tile on both content-script blocks (`/app/enterprise/dashboard*` + `/app/fleets*`) so the apply pipeline can bind to `window.AesStrategyOutcomes` / `window.AesStrategyLearn` without race. Loaded after `default-settings.js` + `diff-plan.js` so `getCurrentWeights()` can resolve `AesStrategy.DEFAULT_WEIGHTS` (set by `decide-routes.js` earlier in the chain).

**Verification.** Manual smoke: reload extension; on `/app/enterprise/dashboard` flip `learningEnabled` ON in the modal's Learning section; tier-flip and apply at least one schedule decision; `chrome.storage.local.get(["aesStrategy:learn:outcomes"])` shows one record with `before` populated and `after: null`. Wait ≥ 24 hours (or call `AesStrategyOutcomes.tryCaptureAfter({minWindowHours: 0, snapshot: window._yourSnap, plan: window._yourPlan})` from the console to bypass the window for testing); the same record now has `after` populated. Repeat with 4 more applies (or seed 5 fake outcomes via console: `await window.AesStrategyOutcomes.record(...)` × 5); click "Run learn cycle" — the toast reads "applied · 5 samples · step 0.05", history list grows by one, current-weights table shifts. Click "Reset weights" → confirm → defaults restored, history grows. `?aes-debug` flag enables 6 in-file smoke tests (3 in outcomes.js for `measure()`, 3 in learn.js for `_gradient()` corner cases) that run on page load without hitting the network.

**v1 deferrals (carried into Slice 15 / 17):** the gradient estimator uses `weeklyResult` delta as the sole reward signal; per-route P&L attribution + ORS delta + load-factor delta layers ship in Slice 15's backtesting harness. Risk-profile presets (Slice 17) will set the step-size + outcome cohort filters per profile. The history ring caps at 52 entries (one game-year on a 4×-real-time server, ~3 months calendar) — a subsequent slice can add an export path to keep older history off-storage.

### Strategy Slice 4 — Apply pipeline + Preview UI + dashboard tile — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Central-hub Tools section (new "Strategy" tile) · full-screen modal openable from the dashboard tile (mounts on `/app/enterprise/dashboard*` and `/app/fleets*`). **Files:** `modules/strategy/default-settings.js` (NEW), `modules/strategy/diff-plan.js` (NEW), `modules/strategy/apply-pipeline.js` (NEW), `modules/strategy/panel.js` (NEW), `modules/central-hub/tiles/strategy-tile.js` (NEW), `manifest.json`.

**First writing PR for the AesStrategy namespace.** Slices 1-3 were pure read; Slice 4 marshals the existing actuators (apply-batch / service-profile-applier / pricing-applier / staff-pilots-applier) behind a tier gate + per-domain enable flags so the user can preview a complete `FleetPlan`, slice it down to a subset of decisions, and apply only the ones they tick. Tier defaults to `preview-only` so every actuator is blocked even after install — three separate flips (tier off preview-only, the per-domain flag for that decision's `domain`, and ticking the decision in the modal) are required for any real POST.

**`AesStrategySettings` (new store).** `settings.strategy` slot (canopy-aware via `currentAccountIdSync()` so multi-account users get isolated settings exactly like `RouteAssistantSettings`). Defaults: `{tier: "preview-only", riskProfile: "balanced", weights: null, crossAirlineEnabled: false, scheduleApplyEnabled/serviceMovesEnabled/priceMovesEnabled/crewMovesEnabled/routeCreationEnabled: false, minOrsTarget: 0.7, routeCreationThreshold: 0.6, priceDeadband: 5, maxPriceMovePerWindow: 10, horizonDays: 7, learningStepSize: 0.05, learningEnabled: false}`. `canApply(s, domain)` is the **single point** that combines tier + per-domain flag; every actuator call site reads it so the safety contract can never drift between caller and store. `domain` ∈ `{schedule, service, price, crew, routeCreation}`.

**`AesStrategy.diffPlan(plan, snapshot)` (new pure function).** Flattens the `FleetPlan` from Slice 3 into a stable, deterministic decision list with one entry per per-aircraft schedule, service move, price move, crew move, and route creation proposal. Each decision carries `{id, kind, domain, title, subtitle, rationale[], payload, applicable, applicableNote}`. `applicable === false` is the engine's self-knowledge that v1 can't actually fire that decision yet — service moves with empty `changes` are advisory until Slice 7's per-category change-set lands; route creation is advisory until Slice 6 ships the actuator; crew moves missing `skillId` are advisory until the staff page has been visited. The Decisions UI shows them all but disables checkboxes for `!applicable` decisions and lights up an "advisory" badge with the why-not note. Stable id format = `<domain>:<keyParts>` so the user's per-decision selection survives re-renders.

**`AesStrategy.apply(plan, opts)` (new orchestrator).** Marshals decisions through actuators in a fixed order (schedules → service → price → crew). Schedule sub-pipeline failure aborts the rest (most disruptive); service / price / crew sub-pipelines fail independently. Schedules go through `AesAfpFleetApplyOrchestrator.start({runs, ctx, source})` — exactly the entry point the wave-overlay save-back uses, so the tier gate inside `apply-batch.js` (autoScheduler.enabled + tier === "apply-on-confirm") still applies. Service moves go through `new RouteAssistantServiceProfileApplier(server, {applyEnabled: true}).apply(profileId, changes, {source})`. Price moves translate the strategy proposer's `toPct` (markets-page percent) to absolute prices by reading `routeAssistant:ticketPrice:<HUB>-<DEST>` cache and multiplying — the applier (`new RouteAssistantPricingApplier(server, {applyEnabled: true, dryRunOnly: false})`) takes absolute integers per class. Crew moves call `new CrewMgmtStaffPilotsApplier().hireOrTrain({server, skillId, amount, mode})`. Returns an `ApplyReport` with `{applied: [{decisionId, domain, ok, result, error?}], skipped: [{decisionId, domain, reason}], totals: {ok, failed, skipped}, aborted, abortReason?}`. Persists every report to `aesStrategy:audit` (ring 500) and the most recent envelope to `aesStrategy:plan:applied` for Slice 5's outcome-attribution.

**`AesStrategyPanel.open(opts?)` (new modal).** Full-screen overlay (z-index 10001, click-outside / Escape to close) with five regions: header (title + tier badge + Refresh + Close), summary chips (one per decision kind + applicable/advisory totals), settings strip (live tier select + per-domain checkboxes that round-trip through `AesStrategySettings.save` and re-render), decisions list (grouped by domain, each row shows title + subtitle + rationale bullets + advisory badge / why-not note, checkbox disabled when `!applicable`), per-aircraft schedule accordion (collapsible per-tail card with leg table). Footer carries the "Apply N selected" CTA — disabled until at least one tick AND tier is not preview-only. Apply threads progress events back into the row decoration so each row gets a green ✓/red ✗ badge with the error text inline. `opts.plan / opts.snapshot / opts.diff` is a passthrough for the dashboard tile's "Compose" preview to skip the second snapshot/allocate round trip.

**`CentralHubStrategyTile` (new tile, section "tools", priority 5).** Body shows: Open-modal CTA, settings strip (tier copy + per-domain dot list), last-apply card sourced from `aesStrategy:plan:applied`, and an inline "Quick plan preview" that composes a fresh plan + diff and shows summary chips inline without opening the full modal — the chip row's "Open full modal →" button hands the already-composed plan to `AesStrategyPanel.open({plan, snapshot, diff})` so the modal renders instantly without re-running the snapshot pipeline. Watches `aesStrategy:plan:applied`, `aesStrategy:audit`, and `settings` storage keys so the tile re-renders after every apply (and after every settings flip in the modal).

**Manifest.** All five new modules registered on both content-script blocks the existing strategy modules use (`/app/enterprise/dashboard*` + `/app/fleets*`), in this order: `default-settings → diff-plan → apply-pipeline → panel` after the existing `allocate-fleet`. The dashboard block additionally registers `central-hub/tiles/strategy-tile.js` after `panel.js`. Tile loads after `central-hub/tile.js` + `tile-registry.js` (lines 115-116) and after every actuator (`apply-batch` is in `aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/fleet-apply-orchestrator.js`, registered as part of the AFP block), so every dependency is resolved by the time strategy-tile mounts.

**Safety contract (additive to §10 invariants).** (1) Strategy never POSTs directly — every actuator call routes through one of the four existing appliers, which carry their own gating (`apply.enabled`, `dryRunOnly`, AFP `tier`). (2) Tier `preview-only` is the **single short-circuit** at the top of `AesStrategy.apply` — no per-domain flag can override it. (3) Per-domain enable flag is required even when tier permits — `AesStrategySettings.canApply(s, domain)` is the only place where both checks happen. (4) `aborted: true` from the schedule sub-pipeline halts the rest of the plan (anything still queued lands in `skipped[]` with `reason: "aborted-upstream"`). (5) Every applied decision lands in the audit ring within the same `apply()` call (no separate flush) — the audit is the source of truth Slice 5 will read.

**v1 deferrals (carried in HANDOVER §9 below).** Service moves with empty `changes` are advisory (Slice 7 fills the per-category change set). Route-creation is advisory (Slice 6 wires the auto-add actuator). Closed-loop learning + outcome attribution lives in Slice 5. Backtest runs against history land in Slice 15. The schedule sub-pipeline is currently **fleet-wide all-or-nothing** because `AesAfpFleetApplyOrchestrator.start` consumes a single `runs[]` — slicing schedules per-aircraft (so the user can apply only some tails) is wired (each aircraft is a separate decision-id in the `decisions[]` list, and the apply pipeline only sends the selected aircraft's `runs[]` to the orchestrator) but the orchestrator's tier-gate-dormant short-circuit will abort the entire batch if the AFP tier hasn't been flipped — flag sits in `aborted` of the report.

**Verification.** Manual smoke (no automated harness): reload extension; open `/app/enterprise/dashboard`; the new "Strategy" tile appears in the Tools section showing tier `PREVIEW-ONLY`. Click "Open strategy modal" → composes a plan against the cached snapshot. Click "Select all applicable" then "Apply 0 selected" — disabled because tier is preview-only and no decisions are ticked. Tick at least one schedule decision — the Apply button label updates but stays disabled with "Tier blocks apply — flip to apply-on-confirm" hint. Flip Tier dropdown to `apply-on-confirm` AND tick `Schedules` flag in the settings strip — Apply enables. Clicking it calls `AesAfpFleetApplyOrchestrator.start` for the selected tail; if the AFP tier (`autoScheduler.enabled` in `aircraftFlightPlan` settings) is dormant the orchestrator aborts and the report carries `aborted: true` with the reason. `chrome.storage.local.get(["aesStrategy:audit", "aesStrategy:plan:applied"])` returns the persisted audit ring + most-recent envelope. `?aes-debug` flag enables 4 in-file smoke tests (one per new module) that verify shape contracts without hitting the network.

### Auto-Pricing — orchestrator pre-apply pass + manifest hot-fix — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel · per-route apply modal + bulk apply modal + Auto-Pricing Tier 3 expander. **Files:** `manifest.json` (orchestrator registration), `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `modules/route-assistant/pricing-applier.js`, `modules/route-assistant/pricing-apply-log.js`, `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`.

Wires `RouteAssistantRouteSync` (the schedule → harvest fnSet → ORS pipeline that already powers the bulk "Sync route + ORS rank" button) into the auto-pricer's apply flow so every Apply runs against fresh data. Without this pass, ORS reads `getOurFlightNumbers` from the legacy `<server><airline>schedule` cache; on a freshly-added route whose flights aren't in that cache, every leg tags as "not ours" and rank flavours come back null — the sandbox projections then silently mislead the user into picking the wrong Δ%. The applier's own GET handshake fetches fresh form context for the POST, so the **POST is always against fresh data** — the leak is in the user's *decision-making*, which the orchestrator pass fixes by anchoring projections to a fresh ORS read at modal-open / pre-Apply time.

**Manifest hot-fix folded in.** `route-sync-orchestrator.js` was untracked in `manifest.json` despite already being instantiated by `_runBulkRouteSync` (panel.js:13363). On a fresh extension install, the existing "Sync route + ORS rank" bulk button would throw `ReferenceError: RouteAssistantRouteSync is not defined`. Registration added to the scheduling content-script block immediately after `ors-scraper.js`; same load order the constructor relies on. The new wiring inherits the same registration. Manifest version bumped 0.6.9 → 0.6.10.

**Settings.** `pricing.apply` gains three fields with deep-merge preservation:
- `refreshBeforeApply: true` — master toggle
- `refreshMaxAgeMinProjection: 5` — skip the orchestrator pass on modal-open / bulk-modal Refresh-visible when both schedule + ORS were last scraped within this window
- `refreshMaxAgeMinApply: 1` — tighter floor for the secondary pre-flight on Apply click (apply commits real money; gets a tighter freshness ceiling than picking-time UI)

`_mergePricing` already deep-merges the apply sub-block, so existing users keep their persisted preference on upgrade.

**Per-route apply modal** (`_openPricingApplyModal`). Three new surfaces:
- **Auto-refresh on modal open.** Spinner overlays the dialog; `_orchestratorPreApplySync([{hub, dest}], {maxAgeMin: refreshMaxAgeMinProjection})` runs once. On resolve, the helper's post-sync `_applyCachedPrices` + `_applyCachedOrs` updates `r.ownPricing` / `r.orsByClass` for the row, the modal's `applyCachedSnapshot()` re-paints the Current column + Δ% display + cache-age note (which flips green for "just now"), and inputs that the user hadn't typed in yet follow to the new current price. Inputs the user has already modified are preserved untouched.
- **"Refresh data" button.** Manual trigger between Cancel and Dry-run preview. Same path as the auto-refresh but ignores the freshness gate (`maxAgeMin: refreshMaxAgeMinProjection` still applied per setting) — useful for the user who paused mid-modal and wants to re-anchor projections.
- **Apply secondary refresh.** Apply click runs `runPreApplyRefresh(refreshMaxAgeMinApply, …)` before the existing `lastApplyAt` / `lastApplyAtGlobal` fetch. Tighter floor (default 1 min) so a real POST always sees a recent orchestrator pass. If the orchestrator halts, the apply is aborted with `code: "preApplySyncHalted"` and the existing `renderResult` flow surfaces the reason. `opts.preApplySync` (the orchestrator's per-route sync envelope) threads into `applier.apply` so the apply-log records the schedule + ORS scrape timestamps that informed this write.

Detached-DOM guards added throughout: `runPreApplyRefresh`, `applyCachedSnapshot`, `renderResult`, and the dryBtn/applyBtn error paths all check `this._pricingApplyModal && this._pricingApplyModal.overlay === overlay` before mutating dialog DOM. Closing the modal mid-pre-flight no longer logs `Cannot read property of null`.

**Bulk apply modal** (`_openBulkPricingApplyModal` + `_runBulkPricingApply`). Two surfaces:
- **"Refresh data before each apply" checkbox** in a new purple-tinted row next to the Δ% inputs. Defaults to `apply.refreshBeforeApply`; modal-local override (so the user can flip one session without changing the global setting).
- **"Refresh visible" button** in the same row. Runs `_orchestratorPreApplySync` over every visible row (filtered through the projection freshness gate so already-fresh rows skip the scrape), then re-collects `cached` references and re-renders the table. Halts surface as a toast warning; the table still re-renders against whatever did sync.

`_runBulkPricingApply` rewritten to run the orchestrator pass over the *selected* routes before the apply loop (only on real applies; dry-run is a pure GET that can't move markets, so refreshing first is wasted work). Halts open the new `_openHaltConfirmModal` sub-modal: "Pre-flight halted at K of N routes — Continue with K synced and skip the rest, or abort the whole bulk?" User picks. On Continue: synced routes apply normally; halt-skipped routes get a synthetic apply-log entry (`status: "skipped"`, `error.code: "preApplySyncSkipped"`) so the audit trail stays symmetric — every selected row lands either an apply entry or a skip entry, no silent drops. Final toast counts `okCount / failCount / skippedCount`.

**Sandbox coordination.** When the orchestrator's post-sync recompute runs and the active sandbox route was in the synced set, `_recomputeOrsSandbox(savedScenario)` re-fires automatically against the freshly-loaded `row.orsByClass` — picking-time projections in the open sandbox card stay in sync with the apply modal's Current column without manual re-trigger.

**`_openHaltConfirmModal({doneCount, totalCount, reason})`** — new sub-modal, z-index 10003 (above the bulk apply modal at 10001 and the apply confirm at 10002). Returns `Promise<"continue" | "abort">`. Esc / outside-click / explicit "Abort" all resolve "abort"; "Continue with K synced" resolves "continue" but is disabled when K=0 (nothing to apply). Mirrors the DOM pattern of `_openBulkApplyConfirmModal`.

**`_orchestratorPreApplySync(pairs, opts)`** — centralised helper used by both apply paths AND the bulk-modal "Refresh visible" button. Lazy-builds `priceScraper` + `orsScraper` (reuses existing instances if already cached), instantiates `RouteAssistantRouteSync`, runs `bulkSync` with progress handle (caller-provided id to avoid collision with `route-sync-bulk` from `_runBulkRouteSync`), filters by freshness gate before dispatch (skipped pairs land in the result map with their existing `scrapedAt` timestamps), and runs `_applyCachedPrices` + `_applyCachedOrs` + `_render` + sandbox recompute on completion. Returns `{results: Map<destIata, {scheduleAt, orsAt, halted}>, halted, reason, doneCount, totalCount, skippedFresh}`. Single source of truth for "run the orchestrator before pricing".

**Apply log enrichment.** `pricing-apply-log.js` `_cleanRecord` preserves a new optional field `preApplySync: {scheduleAt, orsAt, halted}` after `projectedDelta`. Schema is shape-validated by `_cleanPreApplySync` (drops the field entirely if all three subfields are null/false — keeps storage tight). No migration needed; pre-existing entries deserialize with `preApplySync: undefined → null` per the existing null-strip in `_cleanRecord`. `pricing-applier.js` `apply()` threads `opts.preApplySync` into the base envelope between `projectedDelta` and `reason`; every terminal path (`_completeAsVerified` / `_completeAsPostedUnverified` / `_completeAsFailure` / `_completeAsAborted` / `_completeAsDryRun`) carries it via the existing `Object.assign({}, envelope, …)` chain, so the audit trail records the schedule + ORS scrape timestamps that informed each apply.

**Tier 3 expander UI.** `_renderTier3ApplyBlock` gains a "Pre-apply data refresh" checkbox + (when on) two compact tuning fields under it: "Projection freshness (min)" + "Apply freshness (min)". Tooltip explains the orchestrator's role; default values match the settings-store defaults.

**Verification.** Manual smoke (no automated harness):
- Open Route Assistant on `/app/com/scheduling`; confirm the existing "Sync route + ORS rank" button no longer throws on fresh install.
- Right-click a route → "Apply price…"; modal opens with spinner; ~3-8 s later spinner clears; cache-age note flips green to "just now"; sandbox projections (if open) reflect fresh ORS rank.
- Click "Refresh data" inside an open modal: same flow without proceeding to apply.
- Click Apply: secondary pre-flight runs (skipped if data was just refreshed, per the 1-min freshness floor); apply log entry carries `preApplySync.scheduleAt` + `preApplySync.orsAt`.
- Bulk modal: select 3 routes; click "Apply selected"; orchestrator runs over the 3; bulk loop applies against the freshly-loaded prices.
- Force ORS rate-limit → orchestrator halts mid-bulk → halt-confirm sub-modal appears → "Continue with K synced" → halt-skipped routes get `status: "skipped"` log entries with `error.code: "preApplySyncSkipped"`.
- Untouched: Tier 3.2 dry-run (no pre-flight on dry-run), undo toast (snapshot revert, no pre-flight), the existing "Sync route + ORS rank" bulk button (same orchestrator, same `_runBulkRouteSync` path).

### Wave overlay — H slice 3b (interline-store) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel · `/app/com/scheduling*` and `/app/enterprise/dashboard*` (storage available everywhere RA panel mounts). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/interline-store.js` (NEW), `manifest.json`.

Foundation for the per-route interlining/codeshare layer. New `RouteAssistantInterlineStore` (`load`/`save`/`addPartner`/`removePartner`/`clear`/`bulkLoad`/`loadAll`/`totalShare`) keys directional records at `routeAssistant:interline:<HUB>-<DEST>`. Each record is `{partners: [{partnerEnterpriseId, partnerName, productClass (PAX/Y/C/F/CARGO), sharePercent, relationType (INTERLINING/ALLIANCE/CODESHARE/BLOCK_SPACE/WET_LEASE), notes, addedAt}], updatedAt}`. Dedup is on the **composite `(partnerEnterpriseId, productClass)`** key — a single carrier can have separate per-cabin agreements (e.g. cede 30% of Y but block-buy 10 C seats). `removePartner(hub, dest, pid)` without `productClass` removes ALL classes for that partner; with `productClass` only the matching entry. Empty partners arrays drop the storage key (no tombstones).

**Layered above F slice 3 contractual-partners-scraper.** Contractual is the global "who you have agreements with" cache (per-enterprise, non-directional); interline-store is the per-route operational layer. Consumers (3b.1 popover, 3b.2 wave-overlay annotation, profit-estimator integration) read both: contractual gates the partner picker; interline-store carries the share. Kept SEPARATE from `route-overrides-store` because that store's `_clean` is full-replace and would erase interline fields if merged, and because interline records are an array vs overrides' single record per route.

Manifest registered on all three RA content-script blocks (dashboard / scheduling / fleets) immediately after `route-note-store.js`. UI wiring (popover + bulk-view + tooltip annotation + estimator integration) deferred to 3b.1 + 3b.2.

### Daily-driver QoL — U6 inline cell edit + U8 column drag-reorder + Q16 long-op notifications — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel header strip · column headers · table cells · system desktop notifications. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `background.js`, `manifest.json`.

Three independent QoL slices from the §9 Daily-driver UI / interaction backlog, bundled because they all touch the same panel.js file. None changes scoring or scraping behaviour — pure user-experience surfaces.

**U6 — inline cell editing.** New "Overrides" column group (purple-tinted) with three columns: **LFᵒ** (paxLF, 0–1 step 0.05), **Yᵒ** (yieldPerKm, 0–10 step 0.01), **CYᵒ** (cargoYieldPerKgKm, 0–1 step 0.0001). Cell shows the formatted override value in purple with dotted-underline cursor when set, em-dash when unset. Double-click swaps the cell contents for an `<input>`; ⏎ saves through `RouteAssistantRouteOverridesStore.save` wrapped in `_undoableSave` (6-second undo toast); ⎋ cancels (no save); blur saves (deferred 0ms so a click on a different cell can register its own dblclick first). **Empty input clears that single field while preserving siblings** — important because the store's `save` is full-replace; the in-place editor builds a merged record (`Object.assign({}, prev, {field: next})` with explicit `delete merged[field]` on clear) before writing. New `RouteAssistantPanel._renderOverrideCell(td, row, spec)` static + `_beginInlineCellEdit(td, row, spec)` instance method. Cell `title` advertises the keyboard interaction so it's discoverable without a tutorial. Single-cell tweaks now cost 1 dblclick + value + ⏎ instead of 5+ clicks through the full override editor.

**U8 — column drag-reorder.** Each non-frozen `<th>` is now `draggable=true` with HTML5 drag-and-drop handlers (`dragstart` writes the field id to `text/aes-col`; `dragover` lights up the drop target with an `inset 3px 0 0 #a78bfa` left-edge accent; `drop` fires `_reorderColumn(srcField, beforeField)`). The reorderer writes `settings.routeAssistant.columnPrefs.columnOrder` as the full movable-column permutation (so newly-introduced columns inherit a stable position appended at the end on next render). `_activeColumns` permutes movable columns by `columnOrder` after the existing visibility/collapsed-group filtering — frozen `score` + `destIata` stay locked left. `_mergeColumnPrefs` strips any frozen entries on load and dedupes the array so a stale list with a duplicated field doesn't ghost-stack the column. **Sort-on-click survives** because the browser only fires `click` on a drag below the platform threshold; a true drag suppresses the click.

**Q16 — long-op desktop notifications.** New `_notifyLongOpDone(title, message, routeCount)` panel helper sends a `chrome.runtime.sendMessage({type: "aes:notify:long-op", title, message})` to a new background-service-worker handler that calls `chrome.notifications.create` with the AES logo. Wired into 4 long-op completion sites: bulk markets sync, bulk ORS scrape, bulk carriers sync, demand-depth bulk sync. Notification click refocuses the originating tab via `chrome.tabs.update(senderTabId, {active: true})` (gracefully no-op if the tab is gone). `notifications` permission added to manifest. **Threshold is intentionally set inside the panel** (per-call site decides what counts as "long enough") rather than a global setting — different scrapers have different cadences. Dismissed automatically on tab focus or after the OS's default notification timeout.

**Settings shape extension.** `settings.routeAssistant.columnPrefs.columnOrder: []` joins the existing `hiddenFields` + `collapsedGroups` siblings; `_mergeColumnPrefs` returns the third field. No data migration needed — empty array is the documented default.

### Auto-Pricing Tier 3.3a — bulk apply selection table — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel · Auto-Pricing settings expander → "Open bulk apply…" CTA. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`.

Replaces the 3.1-era listing modal (per-row "Open…" buttons) with a real bulk apply pipeline: per-class Δ% inputs at the top, selection checkboxes per row, in-table preview of `current → proposed` prices, cooldown badge per row, confirmation sub-modal with required ack, serial Promise loop dispatching through the same `RouteAssistantPricingApplier` instance the per-route modal uses (so the circuit breaker spans the batch), per-row status feedback. Silent-auto loop (3.3b) deferred — settings remain pre-staged.

**Δ% editor.** One numeric input per class (Y / C / F / Cargo) above the table. Each route's proposed price is `Math.max(1, Math.round(currentCachedPrice × (1 + Δ%/100)))`. Δ% = 0 → proposed = current (the row round-trips at its current value, mirroring the per-route modal's "missing class" semantics). "Match Y across C/F/Cargo" button copies Y's Δ% to the other three for symmetric apply. Routes without a cached price for a given class show "—" and are sent through at AS's current value via the applier's missing-class round-trip rule.

**Selection.** Checkbox column on every row + "Select all" / "Clear" buttons in the bar above the table. Selected rows tinted purple; selection summary in the footer ("N of M selected"). The Apply / Dry-run buttons disable when N = 0.

**Cooldown badge.** On modal open, `_hydrateBulkCooldownMap` reads `getLastSuccessAt(hub, dest)` for every visible row and computes minutes-until-cooldown-clear from `cooldownMinPerRoute`. Routes still in cooldown get an amber "Nm left" badge + 65% row opacity. Selecting them is allowed — the applier's preflight will short-circuit them with `cooldownActive` and the row's status flips to grey "skipped · cooldown".

**Confirmation sub-modal.** On Apply (not Dry-run), a second overlay (z-index 10002) lists every selected route with per-class `current → proposed` lines. Required ack checkbox ("I understand this will POST N price updates to AirlineSim") arms the Apply button. Cancel / Escape / outside-click resolve `false`. Dry-run skips this gate (no real writes to confirm).

**Bulk runner.** `_runBulkPricingApply({selected, deltaPct, scope, dryRun, onRowResult})` iterates serially through `applier.apply(hub, dest, prices, {scope, source: "bulk", submitButton, lastApplyAt, dryRun})`. **Serial by design** — concurrency 1 is the right call because (a) AS Wicket sessions don't parallelise across applies on the same session anyway and (b) a serial loop keeps the circuit-breaker counter monotonic (a 429 storm trips the breaker on row N, every subsequent row returns `breakerCooldown` until reset). One `RouteAssistantPricingApplier` instance is reused across the whole batch so breaker state spans rows. Per-row results pipe back into the table via `onRowResult` and `_summariseBulkResult` translates error codes into compact status badges (`HTTP 429`, `breaker · 8m`, `cooldown`, `applierThrew`, etc.). Final outcome toast — green / amber / red depending on ok/fail mix.

**Tier 3.2 toggle copy reused.** The Apply button's disabled-state title splits across the same two cases as the per-route modal — "Dry-run gate is on" vs "Apply enabled is off" — so the user lands on the same Settings → Auto-Pricing → Tier 3 sub-block via either path.

**Source tag.** Every bulk-launched apply log entry carries `source: "bulk"` (vs `manual` / `sandbox` / `undo`). The audit trail can distinguish per-source; the global apply-log preview in the Tier 3 expander already renders all four equally.

**Verification.** Manual smoke test (no automated harness): with both gates flipped, open "Open bulk apply…", select 3 routes, set Y Δ% to +5, watch the in-table preview show the new prices, click "Apply selected". Confirmation modal lists every change; tick the ack checkbox + Apply. Watch the table fill in with `verified` / `posted` per row, final toast reports the count. Force a 429 — second row's failure trips the breaker, rows 3-N return `breaker · 10m` and stop short-circuiting after Reset. Open in dry-run-only mode → "Dry-run all" path runs the full pipeline (preflight + body construction + audit log entry per row, status `dry-run`) without a single POST.

~~**Deferred (Tier 3.3b).**~~ ✅ **Shipped — see "Auto-Pricing Tier 3.3b — chrome.alarms heartbeat" entry at the top of §1.** Silent-auto loop wires the proposer registry + caps + auto-mute (3.4) behind a `chrome.alarms` driver in `background.js`; per-tick path uses serial `applier.apply()` calls (NOT `_runBulkPricingApply`) so the breaker stays monotonic.

### Auto-Pricing Tier 3.2 — live single-route writes + circuit breaker — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel · Auto-Pricing settings expander + per-route apply modal (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `modules/route-assistant/pricing-applier.js`, `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`. Tech-debt sweep folded in: `modules/route-assistant/schedule-page-scraper.js` (renamed from `ticket-price-scraper.js`), `modules/used-aircraft-scanner/deal-metrics.js`, `modules/used-aircraft-scanner/type-family-map.js`, `content_dashboard.js`, `manifest.json`.

Flips Auto-Pricing from rehearsal-only (3.1) to producing real POSTs to AS's markets-page form, with a circuit breaker mirroring `ors-scraper.js`'s 429/503 pattern, a one-click Undo toast on success, and the per-route + global cooldown enforced from the apply log.

**Default flip + dry-run toggle.** `settings.routeAssistant.pricing.apply.dryRunOnly` default flips `true → false`. Existing users keep their persisted value via the deep-merge in `_mergePricing`. The Auto-Pricing Tier 3 expander gains a new "Dry-run only" checkbox above the "Apply enabled" kill switch — flipping it re-arms the dry-run gate without touching the kill switch. Both gates remain independent (semantic split: kill switch vs slice gate); a real POST still requires `apply.enabled === true && apply.dryRunOnly === false`. `_getPricingApplier` threads the dry-run state into the applier's instance flag, which overrides any `{dryRun: false}` opt the modal might pass — same belt-and-braces convention 3.1 shipped.

**Circuit breaker.** New settings under `pricing.apply`: `circuitBreakerThreshold: 3`, `circuitBreakerCooldownMs: 600000` (10 min), `circuitBreakerTrippedAt: null`, `circuitBreakerHaltReason: null`. Applier gains `_handleRateLimit(envelope, where, status)` (called from both the GET and POST paths when AS responds 429 or 503) which increments `_consecutiveErrors`, fires `onBreakerTrip(reason, trippedAt)` when the threshold is hit, and writes a `code: "rateLimit"` failure entry to the apply log so the audit trail captures every rejection. Counterpart `_resetBreakerCounter()` runs from `_completeAsVerified` and `_completeAsPostedUnverified` and fires `onBreakerReset` — first successful write clears trippedAt. New Step-0 cooldown gate at the head of `apply()` short-circuits to an aborted record (`code: "breakerCooldown"`) when `Date.now() − trippedAt < cooldownMs`; dry-run is exempt since it's a pure GET. Panel persists the trip state via `_persistPricingBreakerTrip` / `_persistPricingBreakerReset` (RouteAssistantSettings.save round-trips), so every panel instance + the next page load honour the cooldown until it expires or the user clicks Reset.

**Breaker banner.** When `circuitBreakerTrippedAt` is set and the cooldown hasn't elapsed, the Tier 3 expander renders a red-tinted banner above the scope chooser with "Circuit breaker tripped" + the halt reason + "~N min remaining" + a Reset breaker button gated on a confirm() prompt. Reset clears trippedAt + haltReason, persists, and re-renders. The banner takes precedence over the existing rationale paragraph so a user opening the expander on a cooled extension immediately sees why Apply is short-circuiting.

**Undo toast.** `renderResult` in the per-route apply modal now branches on `(result.status === "verified" || "posted") && Object.keys(result.prevPrices).length > 0` to fire a 6-second `RouteAssistantToast.success` carrying an Undo action. Click → re-runs `applier.apply(hub, dest, result.prevPrices, {...})` with `source: "undo"`, verifies, toasts the revert outcome. Re-uses the existing applier instance from the original click (closured into the renderer) so the breaker counter + cooldown context survive the round-trip. The previously-shipped `_undoableSave` primitive isn't reused here because pricing applies aren't a single-write semantic — `prevPrices` already round-trips through the `result` envelope and the applier is the canonical revert path.

**Cooldown threading.** `_openPricingApplyModal`'s Apply path now awaits `fetchLastApplyAt() → log.getLastSuccessAt(hub, dest)` before calling `collectArgs(false, lastApplyAt)`; the applier's `preflight({lastApplyAt, cooldownMinPerRoute})` already had the `cooldownActive` blocker logic in 3.1 — slice 3.2 just wires the threading. Dry-run path passes `null` (cooldown only gates real writes).

**Modal + rationale copy de-tiered.** Removed every "Tier 3.1" / "Tier 3.2" string from the panel — the user-facing copy now reads "Dry-run only" / "LIVE writes ENABLED" / "Live writes disabled" with action-oriented wording ("Turn off the dry-run gate plus turn on Apply enabled below to commit real writes"). The Apply button's disabled-state title splits into two cases: dry-run-on vs apply-enabled-off, each with the specific gate the user needs to flip.

**Rate-limit toasts.** `renderResult` adds two new toast branches before the existing failed/aborted/dry-run/success cases: `error.code === "rateLimit"` (warns "Pricing apply halted: HTTP 429 ×N in a row · cooling M min" when `breakerTripped`, errors "rateLimit (HTTP 429, N× in a row)" otherwise), `error.code === "breakerCooldown"` (warns "Pricing apply skipped — circuit breaker cooldown (N min remaining)").

**Tech-debt sweep folded in.** Five long-standing items from §9's Tech-debt list closed in the same diff because they touched the same files as Tier 3.2 (single open + edit, no extra context cost):
- **`ticket-price-scraper.js → schedule-page-scraper.js`** — file rename + class rename (`RouteAssistantTicketPriceScraper → RouteAssistantSchedulePageScraper`) + manifest update + every panel.js call site. Storage prefix `routeAssistant:ticketPrice:` left as-is per HANDOVER §10's stable-key invariant — the rename is class/file only.
- **Family-card grid override-walk** — `TypeFamilyMap.familyList(overrides)` now folds `block.typeFamilyOverrides` into the matching family card so user-redirected types appear in their actual family rather than the grid's "Custom" card. Truly unknown labels still land in Custom.
- **`routeAssistant:topRoutes` freshness on dashboard** — `displayUsedAircraftScanner()` installs a singleton `chrome.storage.onChanged` listener (250ms debounced — RA writes both global + per-hub keys per render) that re-pulls `loadDealContext(server)` and re-renders results. Previous listener removed on each remount via `window._aesTopRoutesListener`.
- **Break-even daily-hours assumption** — `MarketScanDealMetrics._blockHoursFor(row)` picks from `BLOCK_HOURS_BY_CATEGORY` (commuter/turboprop/regional 8h, narrowbody 12h, widebody 14h) keyed off `row.familyCategory`. Falls back to the 10h `DAILY_BLOCK_HOURS` constant when category is missing.
- All four are crossed out in §9's Tech-debt section.

**Verification.** Manual smoke test (no automated harness): reload extension; open the Auto-Pricing expander; confirm the dry-run-only checkbox + Apply-enabled toggle render and persist; right-click a route row → "Apply price…"; in dry-run mode confirm the modal runs the full preflight + body preview + audit log entry without a Network POST. With both gates flipped, the Apply button POSTs the markets-page form, parses verifyOk, fires the Undo toast, and the apply log records `status: "verified"`. Force a 429 by repeated rapid clicks (3 in a row) — breaker trips, banner appears, subsequent Apply clicks short-circuit to `breakerCooldown`, Reset clears the trip. The schedule-page-scraper bulk-sync + Eq/Dep/Wk columns continue to populate (rename had no semantic change). Untouched: existing Tier 3.1 dry-run path, ORS Sandbox bridge to apply modal, sandbox-backtest-store hook (still gates on `sandboxProjected !== null && status ∈ {verified, posted}` per the cohesion-pass slice).

### Track 4 — Slot Optimizer (auto-scheduler preset tweaks) — slices 4a, 4b, 4c, 4d, 4e shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** per-aircraft Flight Plan page (`/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0`) — pure module, no UI; the only user-visible footprint is a 🔧 glyph in the existing preset picker dropdown for tweaked variants. **Files:** `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/slot-optimizer.js` (NEW), `modules/schedule-management/presets-store.js` (extended), `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/wave-applier.js` (4-line surgical patch), `manifest.json`, `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-4a.md` … `AUTO-4e.md` (NEW × 5).

Track 4 is the slot optimizer: take a wave preset whose windows may not match this hub's demand peaks, produce a tweaked preset where wave start times have been shifted to fit the hub's demand profile, and feed the tweaked preset into Track 3's allocator. Slices 4a-4e land sequentially.

**Slice 4a — demand profile per hour.** New `SlotOptimizer.demandProfile({candidates, hubIata})` returns a length-24 histogram `[{hour, demandWeight}, ...]` indexed 0..23 by local hub hour. Each candidate contributes `(paxScore + 0.5 × cargoScore) × weeklyFlights` (weeklyFlights defaults to 1 when missing); the weight is distributed by per-route `frequency` / `hourMask` arrays when present (defensive — checked on the candidate, on `cand._raw`, and on `cand._scoredRow`), with a flat fallback `weight / 24` otherwise. Today's `FlightsFromStore` scrape (`content_flightsFrom.js:560-583`) doesn't expose hour-level frequencies so production runs always go through the flat path; the bucket-aware branch is wired so a future scraper enrichment activates per-hour distribution without touching this module. `?aes-debug` console smoke tests (5 assertions) verify empty input, single-candidate flat formula, default `weeklyFlights`, per-bucket distribution, and bad-entry resilience. Manifest registration sits AFTER the Track 3 auto-scheduler files (`grid-state.js` / `objective.js` / `allocator.js`) since slice 4d depends on them, BEFORE `wave-applier.js`. Tier-gate posture: this slice is pure helper + `?aes-debug` smoke; the production `optimize()` entrypoint is null until 4e ships and will return `{skipped: true, reason: "autoScheduler disabled"}` while `settings.aircraftFlightPlan.autoScheduler.enabled !== true`.

**Slice 4b — slot proposal heuristic.** New `SlotOptimizer.proposeAdjustments({preset, demandProfile})` returns up to 16 `{waves, source, deltaDescription}` proposals via three heuristics: **shift** slides each wave's departure window by ±30/60/90/120 min toward the nearest demand peak (peak chosen by `weight − 0.5×distance` so we prefer heavy nearby peaks over distant heavier ones); **split** halves any wave whose ≥1h-wide window contains a demand valley (defined as an interior hour <0.5× the edge hours' average) into two narrower waves with halved composition; **merge** combines two adjacent thin (<60min) waves whose centres both sit within ±2h of the same hour-bucket peak. `MAX_SHIFT_MIN` capped at ±120min per the plan's "don't blow up the user's intent" guardrail. Helpers `_waveCentreMin`, `_nearestPeakHour`, `_shiftWaveBy`, `_splitWaveAcrossValley`, `_mergeWavesIfStraddlingPeak`, `_halveComposition`, `_addCompositions` are all private — only `proposeAdjustments` is exposed. Verified outside the browser via a Node stub against synthetic demand profiles: a mistuned 14:00 wave + a peak at 17:00 yields 4 shift proposals targeting 17:00; a wave already on the peak yields 0 shifts; a 4-hour wave straddling a clear valley yields 1 split; two adjacent thin waves both within ±2h of a shared peak yield 1 merge. `?aes-debug` smoke tests cover shape, cap, source enum, peak-targeting, empty-preset bail, and the on-peak no-shift case.

**Slice 4c — turnaround-feasibility filter.** New `SlotOptimizer.filterFeasible(proposals, {basePreset, candidates, selectedSpec})` composes each proposal's waves into a synthetic preset (deep-cloning `basePreset.factors` so per-proposal mutations don't leak), runs `ScheduleBuilder.validatePreset()` to hard-drop structural breaks (`presetInvalid` from things like sub-`minTransferMinutes` connection gaps), then runs `ScheduleBuilder.build(routes)` against the OOR-pre-filtered candidate set to surface per-leg `rangeExceeded`/`shortfall`/`routeUnplaced`/`slotViolation` warnings. Each survivor accrues a weighted feasibility score (presetInvalid=10, rangeExceeded=4, shortfall=2, others=1) attached to a fresh `_meta` block. The worst quartile by score is then culled (`Math.ceil(N × 0.75)` kept, minimum 1). Verified via a Node stub loading the real `ScheduleFactors` + `ScheduleBuilder`: 2 valid proposals → 2 survivors with feasibilityScore 1; an invalid proposal (5min gap) → 0 survivors; 4 → 3 after cull. `?aes-debug` smoke tests assert each path. The OOR pre-filter avoids redundant `rangeExceeded` warnings on routes EVERY proposal would fail — only timing-sensitive warnings then drive the score, which is what we want.

**Slice 4d — scoring + selection.** New `SlotOptimizer.selectBest(proposals, {candidates, spec, basePreset, afpCtx, budget})` runs Track 3's `AesAfpAutoScheduler.run` against each surviving proposal and picks the highest-scoring winner. Track 3's allocator resolves the preset via `SchedulePresets.load()` keyed by `o.presetId` with no direct `preset` override hook, so 4d takes the cleanest available path: persist each synthetic preset under a `__aes-auto-tmp-<source>-<ts>` name via `SchedulePresets.create`, call the allocator with the temp's id, then `remove` in `finally` whether the run threw or succeeded. The picker dropdown (4e) will filter out names starting with `__aes-auto-tmp-` so users never see orphans even if a tab dies mid-run. Each run dispatches with `persist: false` so `AesAfpActiveDraftStore` stays untouched until 4e's save path. Sort: descending by `metadata.totalScore`; tie-breaker on `_meta.feasibilityScore` (lower wins) then proposal source enum (shift > split > merge — reflects the plan's "minimal disturbance" preference). Returns `{winner, winnerRun: {build, totalScore, ...}, runs}`. `budget: null` is passed straight through; Track 3's allocator already calls `_fallbackBudget(settings)` when missing, so 4d ships **without blocking on Track 2's `MaintenanceBudget`** as the plan's coordination table requires. Bail-paths covered by `?aes-debug` smoke (empty input → null winner; function-existence). Happy-path is verified end-to-end through slice 4e's `optimize()` against the live page; in-file Node verification of the per-proposal allocator dispatch isn't tractable without the full Track 3 module graph (`AesAfpRouteCandidates`, `AesAfpSpecResolver`, fuel-burn estimator, etc.) all mounted.

### Track 5 — Auto-scheduler preview panel + Apply-all batch pipeline — slices 5a, 5b, 5c, 5d, 5e shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** per-aircraft Flight Plan page (`/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0`) — new `auto-preview` slot in the wide host scaffold, rendered between the toolbar and the candidate table. **Files:** `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/preview-panel.js` (NEW), `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/apply-batch.js` (NEW), `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/auto-apply-log.js` (NEW), `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/host.js`, `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/form-driver.js`, `background.js`, `manifest.json`, `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-5a.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-5b.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-5c.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-5d.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-5e.md` (NEW).

Track 5 is the user-facing surface for the auto-scheduler: render the Track 3 `Build` as a Gantt + per-leg overlay list, then (in slices 5b-5e) commit the whole proposal through the existing background-tab fillAndSubmit pipeline as a single Apply-all batch. Slice 5a ships render-only.

**Slice 5e — audit log + retry queue.** NEW `auto-apply-log.js` (~260 LOC) — `class AesAfpAutoApplyLogClass` exported as singleton `window.AesAfpAutoApplyLog`. Same convention as `audit-log.js`'s per-AFP-aircraft store: dual-store ring buffer with global timeline `aircraftFlightPlan:autoApplyLog` (cap 200) + per-aircraft ring `aircraftFlightPlan:autoApplyLog:<server>:<aircraftId>` (cap 80). Single `chrome.storage.local.set` per `add(record)` keeps both stores in sync (HANDOVER §10 single-writer invariant). `_cleanRecord` normalises IATAs to uppercase, clamps strings, drops nulls. Statuses: `queued | started | ok | failed | aborted | done | error | queue-dismissed`. Public API: `add` / `getRecent` / `getForAircraft` / `getRetryQueue` / `dismissRetryQueue` / `clear`. `getRetryQueue(server, aircraftId)` returns failed entries from the LATEST batch on this aircraft with three exclusions: failed seqs that have a later "ok" entry anywhere in the ring (succeeded on retry or via single-leg apply), all entries when the latest batch carries a `queue-dismissed` marker (user clicked Dismiss; entries stay in the ring for audit history), and duplicate seqs within the same batch (most recent failure wins). EXTENDED `apply-batch.js` (~+90 LOC): every bus emit pairs with a `_logEntry(...)` write — lifecycle entries (`started/done/aborted/error`) carry batch metadata (`total/succeeded/failed/elapsedMs/error`); per-leg entries (`ok/failed`) enrich from `_state.legs[legIdx]` so the audit row carries `origin/dest/depTime/pricePct/service/direction/waveLabel` even after batch state is reset. `_legMetaForIdx(legIdx, seq)` resolves leg metadata by index with a seq-based fallback. Defensive — `add` failures are caught + warned but never block the pipeline. EXTENDED `preview-panel.js` (~+125 LOC): added a `retryQueue` sub-state, `_loadRetryQueue` (called on `ctx:ready`, after every `auto-apply:done`, and after `_dismissPersistedQueue`), `_renderPersistedRetryBanner` (red-tinted banner with `Retry N` + `Dismiss` buttons), `_retryPersistedQueue` (materialises queue entries back into the form-driver leg shape and dispatches via `_applyAll(legs, {source: "retry-persisted"})` — no confirmation modal, this IS the retry), `_dismissPersistedQueue` (calls `AesAfpAutoApplyLog.dismissRetryQueue` then re-renders). `_renderFooter` switches between in-flight / just-finished / persisted-retry / default-tip modes — the persisted retry banner takes precedence over the default tip but yields to in-flight or just-finished. **Manifest fix**: Track 6's slice 6b commit dropped `apply-batch.js` from the AFP content-script block when adding `schedule-diff.js`; this slice restores it AND adds `auto-apply-log.js` ahead of it (`apply-batch.js` calls `_logEntry` which uses the singleton). Final auto-scheduler load order: `grid-state.js → objective.js → allocator.js → schedule-diff.js → slot-optimizer.js → auto-apply-log.js → apply-batch.js → preview-panel.js`. **Track 5 final-deliverable**: end-to-end auto-scheduler flow from "Auto-build week" through "Apply all" through abort/retry now ships behind the `autoScheduler.enabled === true && tier === "apply-on-confirm"` tier gate (defaults disabled / preview-only). The no-programmatic-submit invariant is held: every per-leg POST goes through `AesAfpFormDriver.fillAndSubmit` clicking AS's own green button — the batch pipeline is a sequencing layer over the existing single-leg path, not a new submit path.

**Slice 5d — live progress UI + abort.** Extended `preview-panel.js` (~+410 LOC) — added an `apply` sub-state (snapshot of the in-flight batch), five `auto-apply:*` bus subscriptions (`start` / `progress` / `done` / `aborted` / `error`), and a complete rewrite of `_renderFooter` to switch between three modes: live-progress widget, post-batch summary banner, and the existing default tip. `_renderApplyProgress` paints the in-flight widget — top row with `Applying leg X of N…`, last-result symbol (✓/✗) hover-titled with the per-leg error, ok/failed counters, live `~Xm remaining` ETA, and an Abort button (crimson); progress bar (linear-gradient blue, 200ms width transition); status line carrying the phase label, batchId, and elapsed time (red on `phase: error`). A 1Hz ticker (`_startApplyTicker` / `_stopApplyTicker`) re-renders every second so elapsed/ETA advance smoothly between leg-done events. `_renderApplyResultBanner` paints the post-batch banner — `Done/Aborted/Errored: N ok · M failed · Tm elapsed`, a "M failed — retry" button (only when `failed > 0`) routed to `_retryFailed()`, and a Dismiss button. `_liveSecondsPerLeg` divides elapsed wall-clock by completed leg count once the first leg lands, clamped to `[3s, 20s]`; falls back to slice-5b's `ESTIMATED_SECONDS_PER_LEG = 7` constant before any leg completes. `_abortApply` calls `AesAfpAutoApplyBatch.abort()` (defensive against module-not-loaded). **No appliedLegs rollback needed** — apply-batch.js writes the draft store ONLY on `phase: leg-done` with `ok: true`, so legs that hadn't been confirmed by AS were never written; legs that DID complete before abort stay marked applied (because they really did POST to AS). `_retryFailed` pulls failed seqs from `_state.apply.results.filter(!ok)`, looks them up in the current `lastBuild.flights` (toasts "No matching legs in the current Build — re-run Auto-build first." when the build was regenerated), materialises them with current overlay edits, emits `auto-apply:retry-requested` on the bus + dispatches via `_applyAll(legs, {source: "retry-failed"})` (skips the confirmation modal — the user already acknowledged the original batch). All bus handlers guard on `batchId` mismatch so stale events from a previous batch don't corrupt the active state. Public surface gained `abortApply()`, `retryFailed()`, and an `applyState` getter (defensive copy of `_state.apply`) so the diagnostics console can interrogate progress without poking internals. Phase-1 limitation: progress UI lives in-memory only — page reload restarts blank (the hidden tab keeps running in background.js but the page-side widget doesn't survive); persisting apply state to `chrome.storage.local` for crash-recovery is a Phase-2 enhancement.

**Slice 5c — batch fillAndSubmit pipeline.** Extended `background.js` (~+170 LOC) with a per-aircraft batch submit pipeline that opens ONE hidden tab and iterates legs serially, broadcasting progress back to the originating tab. New constants `AFP_BATCH_FILL_TIMEOUT_MS = 30s`, `AFP_BATCH_RELOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 30s`, `AFP_BATCH_INTER_LEG_DELAY_MS = 500ms` (AS rate-limits Wicket form posts), `AFP_BATCH_TOTAL_TIMEOUT_MS = 20 min` hard cap. New `_afpBatchState` map keyed by `batchId` for abort plumbing. `_afpRunBatchSubmit(req, sender)` — open hidden tab → wait for load → for each leg: send `aes:afp:fill-and-submit` with `leg._batch = {idx, total, batchId, aircraftId}` metadata, wait for AS reload, broadcast progress, sleep 500ms (skipped after the last leg). Reload-timeout recovery: closes the (possibly wedged) hidden tab, recreates a fresh one, waits for initial load, continues to next leg — stops a single bad leg from poisoning the batch. `_afpEnqueueBatch` reuses the existing `_afpSubmitQueues` map so a batch can't race a single-leg Apply on the same aircraft. `_afpBroadcastBatchProgress` uses `chrome.tabs.sendMessage` to the originating tab when known, else `chrome.runtime.sendMessage`; both swallow `lastError` (a navigated/closed origin tab is OK; the batch keeps running in its own hidden tab). Two new `chrome.runtime.onMessage` handlers: `aes:afp:apply-batch` enqueues + responds with the final outcome; `aes:afp:apply-batch:abort` sets the abort flag AND closes the hidden tab so the in-flight `_waitForTabComplete` / `_sendTabMessageWithTimeout` reject promptly. NEW `apply-batch.js` (~330 LOC, registered after slot-optimizer.js + before preview-panel.js so the singleton exists by the time slice 5b's modal calls into it) — vanilla IIFE attaching `window.AesAfpAutoApplyBatch` with `start(payload)` / `abort()` / `state` getter. `start()` re-checks the tier gate via `AesAfpSettings.load()` (slice 5b is the load-bearing gate; this is a backstop), attaches the progress listener BEFORE dispatch, emits `auto-apply:start` on the bus, sends the `aes:afp:apply-batch` runtime message, returns a Promise resolving on completion. `abort()` sends the abort message + emits `auto-apply:aborted`. Per-leg success mirrored into `AesAfpActiveDraftStore.appliedLegs` via `setApplied(server, aircraftId, seq, ts)` so the Fleet Hub overlay tab repaints applied legs in green within ~200ms. Bus events emitted: `auto-apply:start` / `:progress` (one per phase: queued, tab-opened, tab-loaded, leg-start, leg-done, aborted, timeout, done, error) / `:done` / `:aborted` / `:error`. EXTENDED `form-driver.js` (~+25 LOC): `fillAndSubmit(leg)` accepts `leg._batch` metadata for diagnostics overlay hints; defensive top-level `{batch: [...]}` shape rejected with a clear error pointing the caller at the `aes:afp:apply-batch` background message (the page reloads between legs so the form-driver can't carry batch state in-page). `?aes-debug` smoke (4 assertions): public surface + initial state + empty-legs bail. **No-programmatic-submit invariant held**: every per-leg submit still goes through `AesAfpFormDriver.fillAndSubmit` clicking AS's own green button — the SOLE entry point in the codebase that triggers AS form submission, documented in form-driver.js header. The batch pipeline is a sequencing layer over the existing single-leg path, not a new submit path.

**Slice 5b — Apply-all CTA + confirmation modal.** Extended `preview-panel.js` (~+330 LOC) — replaced slice 5a's placeholder hint string with a real Apply-all button next to the Auto-build CTA, plus a new `_openConfirmModal` flow + supporting helpers (`_materialiseLegs`, `_updateModalCounts`, `_closeConfirmModal`, `_applyAll`, `_toast`, `_fmtDuration`). Public surface gained `openConfirmModal()` + `applyAll(legs, opts)` so the diagnostics console can drive the same flow without DOM clicks. Tier gate: button rendered always (so users discover it) but disabled with a per-blocker tooltip unless `lastBuild` is non-null AND `flights.length > 0` AND `autoScheduler.enabled === true` AND `autoScheduler.tier === "apply-on-confirm"` AND `flights.length <= maxLegsPerApply` (default 28). When dormant, an inline hint string under the CTA names the two settings keys to flip. Modal anatomy: header (title + ×), body (subtitle with up-front estimate `legs × ESTIMATED_SECONDS_PER_LEG = 7s` formatted via `_fmtDuration`, alternating-row checkbox list per leg with index/direction/origin→dest/depTime/price%/edit-tag, required I-understand checkbox), footer (counter + Cancel + Apply). Apply turns crimson when armed (selection > 0 AND ack ticked); disabled gray otherwise. Esc / × / dim-background / Cancel all close cleanly. `_materialiseLegs` is the canonical adapter from Track 3's `Build.flights[]` shape (with `depTimeLocal`) to the `{seq, origin, destination, depTime, pricePct, service, …}` shape `AesAfpFormDriver.fill` consumes, merging `draft.perLegEdits[seq]` overlays and falling back to `defaultPricePct` / `defaultService` for missing fields. Bus contract: `auto-apply:requested {ctx, legs, requestedAt, source}` emitted unconditionally on every Apply click, even when slice 5c isn't loaded — gives slice 5e (audit log) a single subscription point. Apply hands off to `AesAfpAutoApplyBatch.start(payload)` when present (slice 5c); otherwise toasts "Apply-batch pipeline not loaded yet (slice 5c)" so the click doesn't appear to silently fail. `RouteAssistantToast` calls are defended via `_toast` (toast-host.js loads earlier in the manifest but IIFE timing isn't strict). **No AS POST in this slice** — invariant held; the actual fill+submit is slice 5c's responsibility. Manifest unchanged (file is already registered from 5a).

**Slice 5a — preview panel render.** New `AesAfpAutoSchedulerPreview` IIFE (`render`, `runAutoBuild`, `lastBuild`) mounts into a new `auto-preview` slot added to `host.js:WIDE_SLOT_NAMES` between `tools` and `candidates`. Six-section root: heading, summary cells (Legs · Weekly hours vs ceiling · Score · Maintenance forecast · Built-when), CTA row (`Auto-build week` / `Re-run auto-build`), status line, Gantt preview via `RouteAssistantWaveOverlay.renderGantt` (same call shape wave-applier uses, so any future renderer improvements come for free), per-leg overlay list with inline editor that mutates `AesAfpActiveDraftStore.perLegEdits[seq]` via `setEdit(server, aircraftId, seq, patch)`, and a footer hint pointing at the tier gate. Maintenance forecast cell color-codes Track 2's `forecastRatio7d`: <100% red / <105% amber / ≥105% green. Edits ALWAYS round-trip through `AesAfpActiveDraftStore` so the Fleet Hub overlay tab repaints via `chrome.storage.onChanged` (same listener wave-applier uses). Validation: origin/dest IATA `^[A-Z]{3}$`, time `HH:MM`, price 1..500; invalid values flag the input red without writing. Bus subscriptions: `ctx:ready` (load settings + draft + budget; render root; attach storage listener), `spec:resolved` (recompute budget), `candidates:updated` (re-enable Build button), `auto-schedule:built` (adopt build payload), `maintenance:scraped` / `wear:updated` (recompute budget). `runAutoBuild()` calls `AesAfpAutoScheduler.run({persist: true})` — Track 3 is a pure compute (no AS POST), so this slice doesn't touch the no-programmatic-submit invariant. **Tier gate posture:** the "Apply all" CTA is a placeholder hint string this slice; slice 5b replaces it with the real button gated behind `settings.aircraftFlightPlan.autoScheduler.enabled === true && .tier === "apply-on-confirm"` (defaults `false` / `"preview-only"`). `?aes-debug` smoke (3 assertions) covers public surface + `_ago` formatter. Manifest registration: `preview-panel.js` sits AFTER the four auto-scheduler files (grid-state, objective, allocator, slot-optimizer) it depends on, BEFORE `wave-applier.js` + `content_aircraftFlightPlan.js`.

**Slice 4e — save tweaked variant + full pipeline entry point.** New `SlotOptimizer.optimize({aircraftId, presetId, candidates?, spec?, ctx?, budget?})` ties slices 4a → 4d into a single async call: load demand profile → propose adjustments → filter feasibility → score the BASELINE (so we can report scoreDelta = winner − baseline) → score each survivor → save the winner via `SchedulePresets.createTweaked`. Returns `{savedPresetId, beforeWaves, afterWaves, scoreDelta, baselineScore, winnerScore, proposalSource, proposalDelta, runs}` on success or `{skipped: true, reason}` on any of the bail paths (tier-gate off, no candidates, no proposals, no feasible survivors, no winner, save failed). Tier-gated: `optimize()` returns `{skipped: true, reason: "autoScheduler disabled"}` without persisting anything when `settings.aircraftFlightPlan.autoScheduler.enabled !== true` — Phase-1 ships disabled per the plan. New `SchedulePresets.createTweaked({base, waves, tweakedFor, nameSuffix?})` piggybacks on `create()` (no fork in the CRUD path) but stamps three provenance fields onto the new record: `tweakedFrom` (base preset id), `tweakedAt` (`Date.now()`), `tweakedFor` (aircraftId). Hub copied from base; `factors` deep-cloned; default name suffix ` · auto-tweaked`. The `wave-applier.js:_renderToolbar` preset picker patch is 4 lines: skip records whose name starts with `__aes-auto-tmp-` (slice-4d scratch presets — defensive against orphans from a crashed run), and prepend `🔧 ` to records carrying a `tweakedFrom` field. Verified via Node stub: tier-gate honoured across three permutations (no settings → "autoScheduler disabled", `enabled: false` → same, `enabled: true` no presets → "SchedulePresets unavailable"); `createTweaked` round-trip persists all three provenance fields plus a copied hub, and the new record appears in the post-save `load()` list. **Open question**: live-page verification of the plan's MCO mistuned-preset spec (waves at 03:00 + 14:00 → tweaked toward 09:00 + 17:00) requires the AFP page + cached FlightsFromStore data + the optimizer toggled on; not run from this agent session — see `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-4e.md` for the manual checklist. Note that without per-route hour buckets in the FlightsFrom scrape (TODO from 4a), the demand profile is flat and the shift heuristic only fires on `weeklyFlights` gaps — the spec's "shift toward 09:00/17:00" effect won't materialise until the scrape enriches.

### Track 6 — Existing-schedule diff + wipe pipeline — slices 6a (+ followup), 6b (+ followup) shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** per-aircraft Flight Plan page (`/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0`) — pure modules, no UI yet (Track 6's UI surfaces land in slice 6d). **Files:** `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/host.js` (rewrote `readVisualFlightPlan` + collapse-helper followup), `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/auto-scheduler/schedule-diff.js` (NEW), `manifest.json`, `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AFP-VFP-parser.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-6a.md` (NEW), `CLAUDE/handover-fragments/AUTO-6b.md` (NEW), and the populated `CLAUDE/...:21944:0?13 MULTIDAYROUTES.html` capture moved into the references folder.

Track 6 makes the auto-scheduler safe for aircraft that already have flights: read the Visual Flight Plan, diff against the proposed Build, present the diff to the user, then (if confirmed) delete and rebuild via Track 5's batch pipeline. Slices 6a + 6b ship the read+compare layer; 6c–6e (delete pipeline, confirmation modal, transactional wipe) are blocked on Track 5's batch pipeline.

- **Slice 6a — VFP parser hardening (+ followup multi-day collapse).** Rewrote `host.js:readVisualFlightPlan` (Slice A's stub shipped against an empty-VFP capture and emitted one entry per *every* block). New parser filters to `.block.flight`, derives `depTimeLocal`/`arrTimeLocal` from CSS `margin-left`/`width` percentages (1% = 14.4 min — the AS positioning is the source of truth, not the unreliable `<span class="start">` text which is HHMM for long bars and just-minutes for short bars), resolves origin from the previous `.block.location > .outbound[title]` IATA and destination from the next `.block.location > .inbound[title]` IATA, captures the AS flight id by parsing `numbers/(\d+)` out of the per-flight overlay anchor, and emits a structured leg with synthetic `seq` (1-based, ordered by `(dayIdx, depTime)`). **Followup change (same file, only `readVisualFlightPlan` + new helper):** added `_collapseDayCrossPairs(legs)` post-pass that pairs `block flight started` legs (one half of a long-haul) with `block flight ended` legs sharing the same `flightId` on `(dayIdx + 1) % 7` — Sun→Mon wrap included — and merges them into ONE logical leg with `crossesMidnight: true`, `arrTimeLocal` from the ended half, `durationMin` summed across both halves, and `destination` pulled from the ended half's same-day next `.inbound[title]`. Validated against the populated 21944 MULTIDAYROUTES capture (CLAUDE/...:21944:0?13 MULTIDAYROUTES.html — 6 day-cross pairs across Mon-Sun): 20 raw flight bars collapse to **14 logical legs** (6 cross-midnight + 8 same-day). Same-day-only 13536 capture (14 flights) and empty-blocks 6968 captures stay unchanged. The retired `spansIntoNext` / `spansFromPrev` flags are stripped from output; only `crossesMidnight` survives. Pathological unpaired halves (a `started` with no matching `ended` or vice versa) emit `console.warn("[AES afp-6a] unpaired day-cross …")` and stay in the output as same-day legs — safe failure mode. 24-assertion Node stub run all green covering empty/null input, same-day legs unchanged, simple paired collapse, multiple pairs, Sun→Mon wrap, unpaired warnings, and the full 14/20 collapse count. Confidence audit in `AFP-VFP-parser.md`: HIGH for flightId/flightLink/flightCode/depTimeLocal/arrTimeLocal/origin/destination after the followup; UNCERTAIN for full flight number (the `.code` span carries only the suffix; airline-prefix lookup deferred to slice 6c). Touched ONLY `readVisualFlightPlan` and added one new helper in host.js — `WIDE_SLOT_NAMES`, `findNewFlightForm`, `findFormTabs`, mount, plus the parallel-track `readSchedule`/`AesAfpVfpReader` integration all untouched. No new manifest entry (host.js was already loaded).
- **Slice 6b — schedule diff engine (+ followup configurable tolerance).** New `AesAfpScheduleDiff.compare(currentLegs, proposedLegs, opts?)` returns `{keep:[{currentSeq, proposedSeq, deltaMin}], delete:[currentLeg], add:[proposedLeg], moveTime:[]}`. "keep" match rule: same origin + destination (case-sensitive 3-char IATA) + `timeDeltaMin(depTimeLocal) ≤ tolerance` (default 15), where `timeDeltaMin` is the shorter-around-midnight distance (so 23:55 vs 00:05 → 10 min not 1430 min). When more than one proposed leg matches a current leg's `(O, D, depTime±tol)` window, the smallest absolute time delta wins. Each proposed leg can be claimed at most once per `compare()` call; greedy on the current side. **Phase-1 simplification (permanent, retired open question):** when O/D matches but depTime delta is OUTSIDE the window, the pair gets split into delete+add — `moveTime` is always `[]` (kept in the return shape so a future slice can flip the heuristic without breaking callers). Reasoning: AS's edit-flight Wicket POST is more fragile than the delete-then-create path; reusing Track 5's apply pipeline via delete+add costs one extra POST per moved leg but lets 6c–6e ship without a separate edit-flight reverse-engineering session. **No day-mask expansion**: this slice compares legs as-passed; callers wanting per-day apples-to-apples comparison must pre-expand `dayMask` (slice 6d's confirmation modal is the right place). Safe-failure: non-array inputs become `[]`; legs missing/malformed `origin`/`destination`/`depTimeLocal` go to `delete` (current side) or `add` (proposed side). **Followup change:** `compare` accepts a 3rd `opts` arg with shape `{toleranceMin?: number}`. New `_resolveTolerance(opts)` picks the active value by precedence — caller wins (finite ≥ 0), then `window.AesAfpSettings.cached?.()...autoScheduler.diff.toleranceMin` (sync read, only consulted when a future slice registers `cached()` for the apply pipeline to pre-warm), falling back to the `TOLERANCE_MIN` constant (15). Defensive non-finite/negative values at any layer fall through to the next so a misconfigured setting never breaks the diff. Settings shape `settings.aircraftFlightPlan.autoScheduler.diff.toleranceMin: 15` declared as a working-tree edit to `settings-extension.js` (defaults block + `_mergeAircraftFlightPlan` deep-merge via `numFieldNonNeg` + `save` deep-merge so a partial save doesn't blow away sibling knobs); the file itself is still untracked at this followup, so the addition lands when whoever owns slice F next commits it. Until then `_resolveTolerance` falls through to `TOLERANCE_MIN` (identical 15), so the diff is bit-for-bit compatible with slice 6b for callers not passing the opt arg. UI to expose the slider is deferred to slice 6d's confirmation modal — declared field gives 6d a stable target. Public surface also exposes `timeDeltaMin(a, b)`, `isMatchable(leg)`, and the read-only `TOLERANCE_MIN: 15` constant for callers + tests. Verified via 34-assertion `?aes-debug` smoke block — 30 from slice 6b (empty/empty, identical lists, ±14 within window, ±30 outside, different-dest, lowercase-IATA rejected, ambiguous-multiple closest-wins, duplicate-current single-claim, midnight wrap) plus 4 from the followup (`{toleranceMin: 5}` rejects ±10min, `{toleranceMin: 60}` keeps ±30min, `{toleranceMin: 0}` only keeps exact-time, `{toleranceMin: -1}` falls back to default 15) — all green under both browser load and a Node stub run (`global.window = {location:{search:"?aes-debug"}}` → 34/34). Manifest registration: `schedule-diff.js` sits AFTER Track 3's `allocator.js`, BEFORE Track 4's `slot-optimizer.js` per the slice spec.

### Track 1 — AFP form-driver hardening + selector audit + diagnostics overlay — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** per-aircraft Flight Plan page (`/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0`), gated on the `?aes-debug` query flag for the overlay slice. **Files:** `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/form-driver.js`, `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/host.js`, `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/diagnostics.js`, `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/SELECTORS.md` (NEW).

Fixes the long-standing "I clicked a candidate but the AS dropdowns didn't change" bug. Root cause: AS uses **select2 v3** (the origin/destination selects carry the `select2-offscreen` class — a v3-only marker; v4 uses `select2-hidden-accessible`); v3's `val` setter requires `triggerChange=true` as the third argument or the visible chip stays on "Choose One" while `<option selected>` is silently correct underneath. Our previous code called `$sel.select2("val", value)` with no third arg inside a try/catch that swallowed the v3-vs-v4 signature mismatch, so the chip never repainted. Submit-from-this-state was a footgun.

**Slice 1a — select2 v3/v4 commit fix.** New `_detectSelect2Version()` helper inspects `$.fn.select2.amd` (v4) vs `$.fn.select2.defaults.matcher` (v3). `_commitSelect` now branches on the detected version: v3 → `$sel.select2("val", value, true)` (third arg = triggerChange, repaints chip); v4 → `$sel.val(value).trigger("change")`; unknown → both. Errors no longer silently swallow — they route to `_diag("commit-select-error", ...)` for visibility through the diagnostics overlay (slice 1c). The native `dispatchEvent("change")` fallback survives for the no-jQuery case. The airport-id concern from the planning prompt is verified-correct without code change: `_findOptByIata` matches against the option's visible label `"City, Region (IATA)"` via `\b<IATA>\b`, then `_commitSelect` reads `opt.value` (the airport id, what AS Wicket expects on POST). No semantics change there.

**Slice 1b — "New Flight Number" tab activation.** The form's submit button only exists in the New tab's panel — when the user is on the "Existing Flight Number" tab, `findNewFlightForm()` returns null and form-driver bails. New `host.js:findFormTabs()` locates both tab anchors (text-match on "New Flight Number" / "Existing Flight Number" since Wicket ids reshuffle every render; href substring `'newFlightNumber'` / `'toggle~new'` is the fallback) and reports `activeTab` as `"new" | "existing" | "unknown"`. Exposed on `window.AesAfp.getFormTabs`. Form-driver's new `_ensureNewTabActive()` clicks the New anchor when the active tab is "existing" and polls `findNewFlightForm()` every 100ms up to 1.5s for the panel swap; timeout records `tab-activate-timeout` and falls through to the existing form-not-found error path. `fill(leg)` is now `async` and awaits this gate; `fillAndSubmit` likewise. Both internal callers (`bus.on("candidate:selected")` and the toolbar "Fill latest pick" button) are fire-and-forget so the async conversion is invisible to them. The single external caller (`content_aircraftFlightPlan.js`'s `aes:afp:fill-and-submit` handler) was already promise-based via `.then(...)`, so the API surface didn't move.

**Slice 1c — diagnostics overlay.** Extends the existing `diagnostics.js` (which previously logged bus events to `console.groupCollapsed` only) with a fixed-position floating panel rendered when `?aes-debug` is set on the URL or `localStorage["aes-afp-debug"] === "1"`. Five sections: header (with × that sets the LS flag to "0" and removes the panel), live form-state table (six rows — name, value, chip text — polled every 500ms; reads chip text from `.select2-chosen` for v3 selects and from `option.text` for native ones; surfaces `"form not in DOM (active tab: ...)"` when the New tab isn't active), last-fill JSON (memoed from `candidate:selected` + `form:filled` bus events), recent-events tail (last 8 from the existing 200-cap ring), and three buttons — `[Force re-sync]` re-emits the memoed `candidate:selected` payload through the bus (exercises the full path including form-driver's listener), `[Run self-test]` walks the SELECTORS table from slice 1d, `[Clear log]` empties the events ring. New public surface on `window.AesAfpDiagnostics`: `record(kind, payload)` (consumed by form-driver's `_diag` shim, which is a no-op when diagnostics isn't loaded — zero production cost), `runSelfTest()`, `forceResync()`, `clearLog()`, `memo`, `SELECTORS`. `record` accepts both 2-arg `(kind, payload)` and the existing 3-arg `(kind, name, payload)` shapes; the wrapBus internals continue using the 3-arg form.

**Slice 1d — SELECTORS.md + self-test.** New `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/SELECTORS.md` (first per-module selector reference doc — convention going forward for modules with select2 / Wicket exposure). Twelve selectors documented across four scopes: form root, six selects (origin/destination select2 v3, four native), reverse anchor, two tabs, hidden Wicket fields. Each entry pairs primary + fallback selectors plus the version-specific notes (e.g., select2-offscreen is v3, option values for origin/destination are airport ids not IATA). The runtime mirror is the `SELECTORS` table in `diagnostics.js` — `runSelfTest()` walks it, querying with the right scope (form-rooted via `findNewFlightForm()`, tabs via `findFormTabs()`'s purpose-keyed dispatch since the tab anchors aren't pure CSS-selectable, page-level via `document.querySelector`) and returns `{purpose, selector, ok, count, note}` per row. Overlay's "Run self-test" button renders pass/fail inline.

**Slice 1e — validation pass.** Manual checklist (no automated test — the bug is purely visual select2 chip repaint):
1. Open `/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0?aes-debug` — the diagnostics overlay appears top-right.
