2. Click "Run self-test" — every row green on the New tab.
3. Click 5 distinct candidate rows (or apply 3+ wave legs); each click should repaint all six form chips with the new values. The overlay's live form-state table reflects each commit within 500ms.
4. Switch to the "Existing Flight Number" tab and click a candidate — form-driver auto-flips to the New tab within ~300ms and fills normally; overlay logs `tab-activate` + `tab-activate-ok`.
5. Click "Force re-sync" — the last `candidate:selected` re-runs through the bus and the form re-fills.

**Verification.** Internal smoke (no live AS access from this session): the diff is structurally consistent (form-driver / host / diagnostics co-evolved; SELECTORS.md mirrors the runtime table). Live verification is the user's manual pass per the checklist above; any residual gaps go into a follow-up fragment in this section.

**Deferred (out of scope for this track).** Submit button state isn't surfaced in the overlay (would need an extra row reading `f.submitBtn.disabled` plus a hover-test for cursor: not-allowed) — easy to add if the user wants it. The overlay isn't draggable; if it covers something the user needs, dismiss with × and re-enable via `?aes-debug=1`. Selectors for adjacent surfaces (route-assistant panel, schedule-management) aren't documented yet — recommend mirroring the SELECTORS.md template per module as a future hygiene pass.

### AFP Schedule Control (Fleet Hub) — Tier 1 dry-run shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Fleet management page (`/app/fleets*`) — new **D** action chip per aircraft row alongside R/S/P. **Files:** `modules/aircraft-flight-plan-dashboard/` (NEW directory, 9 files), `modules/fleet-hub/inline-table.js`, `modules/fleet-hub/host.js`, `manifest.json`.

First slice of the AFP "schedule control" surface that the user wired this session. Goal: compose a wave plan + create flight numbers via proxy GET → POST WITHOUT navigating into each aircraft's Flight Plan page, plus a deep-link affordance that preserves the `?<flightNumberId>&aes-debug` URL pattern. **This slice ships dry-run only** — `AesAfpFnApplier` mirrors `RouteAssistantPricingApplier`'s `dryRunOnly:true` + `applyEnabled:false` double-gate convention, both gates hard-coded `true`/`false` respectively; `apply()` always returns `{status: "dry-run", ...}`. Tier 2 will introduce the live POST + verify + undo path; Tier 3 the bulk multi-aircraft loop.

**New module — `modules/aircraft-flight-plan-dashboard/`.** Nine files, all isolated from the existing AFP slices to preserve the AFP form-driver "never POSTs" invariant (HANDOVER §10): (1) `flight-number-apply-log.js` — global ring `aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog` (cap 200) + per-aircraft ring `aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog:<server>:<aircraftId>` (cap 20); 5-min dedup on `(fingerprint+status)` mirrors `pricing-apply-log.js`. (2) `proxy-page-fetcher.js` — `fetchAircraftFormContext(aircraftId)` GETs `/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0` with `credentials: "include"`, parses via the applier's static helper, 60-sec in-memory cache. (3) `flight-number-applier.js` — the gold-standard mirror of `pricing-applier.js`. `parseFormContext(html)` lifts `host.js:findNewFlightForm()` onto a static helper that walks a DOMParser doc; `buildBody({formContext, leg})` composes the URL-encoded body via `_findOptByIata` (option text \b<IATA>\b, same regex as `form-driver.js:_findOptByIata`) + value matchers for hours/minutes/price/service; `apply()` short-circuits to dry-run after preflight + body composition. (4) `candidate-pipeline.js` — wraps `AesAfpRouteCandidates.compute` + `AesAfpWaveApplier.buildFromCandidates` for off-page invocation. Both wrapped APIs are headless-safe (route-candidates' `bus.emit("candidates:updated")` is guarded by `if (window.AesAfp && AesAfp.bus)` at line 152; wave-applier's `buildFromCandidates` calls `RouteAssistantWaveOverlay.buildSchedule` directly with no slot dependency). (5) `deep-link.js` — `AesAfpDashboardDeepLink.buildAircraftUrl(server, aircraftId, flightNumberId)` constructs the `?<fnId>&aes-debug` form. (6) `settings-extension.js` — Tier 1 hard-coded gates; `save()` is a no-op + warn so storage editing can't unlock writes. (7) `fleet-roster-view.js` — thin adapter over `FleetHubAircraftAggregator.enrich()` with a 30-sec cache. (8) `panel.js` — fixed-position overlay (z 99999) opening as a 560px modal at top-right; sections: header (reg/equipment/loc + "↗ Open in AS" deep-link + ×), preset picker + Generate, status line (hub · destinations · already-scheduled · candidates · build legs), build preview table with per-leg "Dry-run" buttons that inline-expand the would-be POST body, "Recent applies (last 10)" expander reading from the per-aircraft ring, gate footer ("🔒 Tier 1 — dry-run only"). (9) `host.js` — singleton on `/app/fleets*`, lazily wires the dependency graph on first D click and opens the panel.

**Modified files.** `modules/fleet-hub/inline-table.js` — added `D` to `EVENT` + `MARKER` static maps, added the per-row chip in `_actionsCell`, added the click branch in `_onClick`. `modules/fleet-hub/host.js` — added `_dashboardHost` lazy field, added the `E.D` listener in `_bindTableListeners`, added `_onActionD(detail)` that lazy-instantiates `AesAfpDashboardHost` and calls `openFor(detail)`. `manifest.json` — appended 13 dependency scripts + 9 new dashboard scripts to the existing `/app/fleets*` `content_scripts` entry. **De-duplication checked** per HANDOVER §10 (the AFP-zero-tab invariant): cross-checked against the existing `/app/fleets*` block — `state-store`, `active-draft-store`, `submit-bridge`, `range-buckets`, `presets-store`, `schedule-store`, `schedule-builder` are NOT re-listed. AFP `host.js` is loaded here (it's idempotent — line 32 `if (window.AesAfp) return`; `mount()` bails on missing `.as-page-aircraft`); we DO NOT load `content_aircraftFlightPlan.js` (which would call `mount()`), and we DO NOT load AFP `form-driver.js` (which has its own toolbar render path that would silently disable on missing slots).

**Storage delta.**
```
aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog                       # global ring (cap 200)
aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog:<server>:<aircraftId> # per-aircraft ring (cap 20)
aircraftFlightPlanDashboard:settings                # T1 reads ignore stored values; gates locked
```

**Verification.** Manual smoke test on `/app/fleets*`: D chip renders alongside R/S/P; click D → modal opens with header + preset dropdown; "↗ Open in AS" link constructs `https://<server>/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0?aes-debug`; Generate runs the proxy GET (verify in DevTools Network — single GET, no POST), candidate pipeline produces a build, per-leg "Dry-run" buttons append a `dry-run` entry to `aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog:<server>:<aircraftId>` and inline-expand the body preview. **Critical T1 invariant:** ZERO POSTs in the Network tab during any interaction with the modal. Reload `/app/fleets*` → D chip and modal flow survive Wicket re-renders.

**Deferred (out of scope for this slice).** Tier 2 (live single-leg POST + verify + undo behind explicit double-gate flip), Tier 3 (bulk multi-aircraft + cooldowns + circuit breaker), Tier 4 (silent-auto via `chrome.alarms`), delete/replace existing flight numbers (create-only is the user-locked scope).

### Sitewide brutalist skin + QOL — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** every AS page (`/app/*`, `/action/*`). **Files:** `modules/site-skin/bootstrap.js` (NEW), `modules/site-skin/table-polish.js` (NEW), `modules/site-skin/keyboard-shortcuts.js` (NEW), `modules/site-skin/density-toggle.js` (NEW), `modules/site-skin/click-to-copy.js` (NEW), `modules/site-skin/breadcrumb.js` (NEW), `css/skin/skin-global.css` (NEW), `css/skin/skin-finance.css` / `skin-fleet.css` / `skin-schedule.css` / `skin-info.css` / `skin-ops.css` (NEW), `manifest.json`, `options.html`, `options.js`, `modules/aes-menu.js`.

Extends the brutalist treatment from AES-injected surfaces (Route Assistant, AES menu, About dialog, status strip) to AirlineSim's *native* Wicket+Bootstrap GUI sitewide. Plus four QOL features that reuse the same primitives.

**Activation.** Opt-in toggle persists in `chrome.storage.sync` under `aes_skin_enabled` (default `true`) and `aes_skin_density` (default `comfortable`). The bootstrap script runs at `document_start`, reads both values synchronously into a `chrome.storage.sync.get` callback, and stamps `<html data-aes-skin="on|off" data-aes-density="comfortable|compact" data-aes-page="<kind>">` before AS paints. Body class `aes-skin` is mirrored as soon as `<body>` is present (MutationObserver fallback). `chrome.storage.onChanged` reapplies the attributes within ms of a toggle — no reload needed. The bootstrap also exposes `window.AESSiteSkin` (`isEnabled` / `getDensity` / `getPageKind` / `setEnabled` / `setDensity` / `cycleDensity` / `showShortcuts` / `hideShortcuts`) so other modules read/write through one helper instead of re-implementing storage glue.

**Override layer.** `css/skin/skin-global.css` (~600 LOC) restyles every AS Bootstrap primitive scoped to `body.aes-skin`: typography baseline, `.btn` family (incl. `.btn-primary` → rust, `.btn-success` → moss, `.btn-warning` → amber, `.btn-danger` → crimson outline-fill on hover, `.btn-info` → cobalt), `.form-control` / labels / `.input-group-addon` / `.help-block`, `.panel*` with the signature double-rule seam under `.panel-heading`, `.well`, `.alert*` (left-edge status stripe), `.navbar` / `.navbar-default` / `.nav-tabs` / `.nav-pills` / `.breadcrumb`, `.table` / `.table-striped` / `.table-hover` / `.table-bordered` (oxide thead, paper-rule rows, bone-2 stripe), `.modal-content` / `.modal-header` / `.modal-footer` / `.close` / `.modal-backdrop`, `.dropdown-menu` (folded in from former `content.css` legacy block), `.badge` / `.label*`, `.pagination`, `.progress*`, `.popover` / `.tooltip-inner`, `.list-group`, AS's own `.as-panel` / `.as-fieldset` / `.as-table-well`. Density compact-mode reduces padding on tables/forms/buttons via `html[data-aes-density="compact"]` selectors. Five per-page polish files (`skin-finance.css`, `skin-fleet.css`, `skin-schedule.css`, `skin-info.css`, `skin-ops.css`) scope to `body.aes-skin[data-aes-page="…"]` for narrow tweaks (right-aligned currency, mono regs, schedule tab quirks). Every rule excludes `#aes-route-assistant` so the RA panel's dark theme is undisturbed. Zero hardcoded hex/px — pure tokens from `design-tokens.css`.

**QOL #1 — table polish.** `modules/site-skin/table-polish.js` (~95 LOC). Scans every `<table>` on `DOMContentLoaded` and after each AS Wicket AJAX rerender (debounced MutationObserver, 200 ms). Tables with ≥8 rows (and not inside an AES-injected panel or RA root) gain `.aes-skin-sticky` so CSS pins `<thead th>` via `position:sticky`. Tables with ≥12 rows additionally get a brutalist filter input above them (`.aes-skin-filter-wrap` — mono caps "FILTER" label + search input + "N ROWS" count badge). Typing hides non-matching rows via `requestAnimationFrame`-batched textContent substring match. Filtered count updates live.

**QOL #2 — keyboard shortcuts + density toggle.** `modules/site-skin/keyboard-shortcuts.js` (~140 LOC). Vim-style two-key sequences with 800 ms prefix timeout: `g d` dashboard, `g f` fleets, `g s` scheduling-for-current-hub, `g a` accounting, `g l` leasing, `g i` inventory-for-current-hub, `g m` markets-for-current-hub, `g o` ops, `g e` enterprise-info-for-current-id, `g x` settings. `/` focuses the first visible filter/search/text input. `?` opens a centred brutalist help popover (`.aes-skin-help` — uses the `.aes-modal__close` primitive for parity). `Esc` closes the help; otherwise listeners are no-ops while the user is typing in a field. `Ctrl/Meta/Alt` chords pass through so browser shortcuts aren't hijacked. `Shift+D` (separate file `density-toggle.js`, ~25 LOC) cycles `comfortable ↔ compact` via `AESSiteSkin.cycleDensity()`.

**QOL #3 — click-to-copy.** `modules/site-skin/click-to-copy.js` (~90 LOC). Single delegated `mouseover`/`click` listener on `document.body`. A cell becomes `.aes-skin-copyable` (cursor:copy + dashed rust outline on hover) when its trimmed text matches: currency (`-?(?:[A-Z]{1,3}\$|\$|€|£)?\s?-?\d{...}`), 3-letter uppercase IATA, or aircraft registration (`X-XXXX` style — fleet pages only). Click → `navigator.clipboard.writeText` (with `execCommand` fallback) → 1.4 s success toast (`.aes-toast--success` lazily mounted on a sub-host so it doesn't compete with RA's own toast host). Skips cells inside `<a>`/`<button>`/inputs/`#aes-route-assistant`/`.aes-panel`.

**QOL #4 — breadcrumb strip.** `modules/site-skin/breadcrumb.js` (~70 LOC). Parses `location.pathname` against a small map and renders `nav.aes-skin-breadcrumb` (`position:sticky; top:0; z-index:5` so it sits below `.modal-backdrop` at 1040) as the first child of `<body>`. Format: UPPERCASE display caps with `paper-rule` slash separators, last crumb in rust as the leaf. Page kinds mapped: finance (accounting/leasing/capital/assets/cashflow), fleet (fleets list + aircraft detail with reg parsed from title), scheduling-`<HUB>`, inventory-`<HUB>`, markets-`<HUB>`, aircraft-market, info/airport-`<id>`, info/enterprise-`<id>`, ops/stations, dashboard, settings, flight-info, staff. Skipped on the scheduling page family because the Route Assistant panel already provides chrome there.

**AES menu wiring.** `modules/aes-menu.js`'s `#createMenuItem` factory gained two new prop types: `onClick` (function — bound to the inner anchor's click) and `stateLabel` (function — appends a live mono-caps badge that gets re-stamped via `chrome.storage.onChanged` listener attached to the menu). Three new top items added above the existing "Community" header: `Brutalist Skin [ON|OFF]`, `Density [COMFORT|COMPACT]`, `Shortcuts` (opens the same `?` help popover via `AESSiteSkin.showShortcuts()`).

**Options page.** `options.html` gained a brutalist settings panel above the data display: a single skin checkbox + comfortable/compact density radio. `options.js`'s new `wireSiteSkinSettings()` reads the same `chrome.storage.sync` keys, writes through on change, and listens for changes to keep the UI in sync if the user toggles via the AES menu while the options tab is open.

**Manifest topology.** Two global content_scripts entries on `app/*` + `action/*`:
- Entry A (`run_at: "document_start"`) — all six skin CSS files + the existing `design-tokens.css` / `components.css` / `content.css` + `bootstrap.js`. Loading at document_start prevents flash of un-skinned UI.
- Entry B (default `document_idle`) — jquery, helpers, design-tokens.js, aes-menu.js, about-dialog.js (existing), then the five new QOL JS modules (keyboard-shortcuts, density-toggle, table-polish, click-to-copy, breadcrumb).

**What's untouched.** Route Assistant panel (`#aes-route-assistant` + `data-aes-theme="dark"`) — its own dark surface and primitives stay. AES-injected panels using `.aes-panel` / `.aes-modal` / `.aes-toast` — those primitives in `components.css` are untouched. Existing accounting/finance AES panels — pass through unchanged. Wicket form behaviour — every override is style-only; no AS classes renamed/removed.

**Verification.** Manual smoke test (no automated harness in this extension): reload extension, visit each AS page family (dashboard, fleet, finance×5, scheduling, inventory, markets, info, ops, settings), confirm panel chrome / tables / forms / modals render brutalist; toggle skin off in options and confirm reversion within ~50 ms; long table sticky header + filter; `g f` navigates; `Shift+D` cycles density; click an AS$ figure → toast confirms copy; press `?` → help popover. RA scheduling page should still render its dark panel without regression.

### Cohesion pass — Aircraft Flight Plan Assistant wire-up + Tier 3 ↔ back-test loop — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** per-aircraft Flight Plan page (`/app/fleets/aircraft/<id>/0`) + Route Assistant Auto-Pricing apply modal. **Files:** `manifest.json`, `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`.

Closed two integration loops left open by the prior sessions' work. No new features — purely "make the latest deliverables actually reach the user."

**Loop 1 — AFP wire-up.** The eight slice files in `modules/aircraft-flight-plan/` (host, spec-resolver, route-candidates, form-driver, wave-applier, audit-log, settings-extension, state-store) were committed but the manifest only registered Slice A (`host.js`) for `/app/fleets/aircraft/*/0*`. Slices B-F never loaded; none of the `RouteAssistant*` stores they read defensively loaded either. The AFP `content_scripts` entry now declares the full dependency block: 11 RA modules (settings-store, fleet-store, demand-store, score, distance-resolver, type-specs-store, route-overrides-store, route-note-store, watchlist-store, wave-overlay, toast-host) + flightsfrom data-store + aircraft-type-specs + 7 AFP slice files (settings-extension before host so AesAfpSettings is available, then host so the bus exists, then audit-log/spec-resolver/route-candidates/form-driver/wave-applier which all subscribe to the bus at load time), then the `content_aircraftFlightPlan.js` entry script. **Critical de-duplication:** `state-store.js` and the four `schedule-management/*` modules used by wave-applier are NOT included in the AFP block because the user's `/app/fleets*` content_scripts entry already loads them on this URL — listing them in both entries would re-execute the `class` declarations and throw. Cross-checked via Python set-diff on the three matching entries (generic `/app/*`, fleets-star, AFP-zero-tab): zero file overlap, 40 distinct files inject on the AFP URL. AFP module is now reachable end-to-end on the per-aircraft Flight Plan page.

**Loop 2 — Tier 3 dry-run apply → back-test store.** The Tier 3.1 apply path defined a `trigger: "tier3-apply"` value in `sandbox-backtest-store.js`'s entry schema but never invoked `RouteAssistantSandboxBacktestStore.log(...)`. Added the call inside `_openPricingApplyModal`'s `renderResult` closure (panel.js ~13420). Three changes: (1) the modal launch site (`_buildOrsSandboxScenarioCard` → "Apply this scenario as price…" button) now passes `sandboxProjected` (full projected envelope) and `sandboxModelParams` (the projection's model params snapshot) alongside the existing `sandboxScenario` + `projectedDelta`; (2) `_openPricingApplyModal` captures both as closure variables; (3) `renderResult` logs a back-test entry gated on **(a)** `sandboxProjected` being present (only sandbox-launched applies — manual row-context-menu applies have no projection to compare against) AND **(b)** `result.status` being `verified` or `posted` (real writes — dry-run is skipped because the back-fill loop would otherwise associate the projected share with a marketShare snapshot whose prices never actually changed, corrupting slice 3c's bias/RMSE metric). Entry shape mirrors the calibrate-T entry (ts, trigger, scenario, modelParams, projected: {share, paxPerWeek, revenuePerWeek, profitPerWeek}). Back-fill via `_runBulkMarketScrape` → `backfillManyFromMarketShares` is automatic (the trigger string is irrelevant to back-fill; only ts + missing observed.share matter). Slice 3c's "Model fit" row in the settings drawer immediately starts including Tier 3 entries alongside calibrate-T entries once Tier 3.2 ships and real applies start flowing. **Today**, while both gates are blocked, no entries flow yet — the wire is laid for the next slice.

**Toast cross-page continuity.** AFP's audit-log slice (Slice F) emits toasts via `RouteAssistantToast.show()`; the manifest expansion above includes `toast-host.js` so the toast container mounts on the AFP page. The N2 notification bell stays panel-scoped by design (HANDOVER §10 — "N2 popover is not a toast itself … Outside-click + Escape teardown is local"). Cross-page behaviour: AFP toasts flow through `RouteAssistantToast._history` (in-memory, ephemeral) and become visible via the bell on the user's NEXT visit to the RA panel. Closing the AFP tab clears that history slice.

**VFP parser scope.** Validated `host.js`'s outer Visual Flight Plan selector chain (`.as-panel.visual-flight-plan .vfp.vfp-main .day .blocks`) against the captured `/app/fleets/aircraft/6968/0` snapshot — 7 `.day` elements with `.blocks` children, exactly matching `host.js:175`. The captured sample has empty `.blocks` so the per-flight-bar inner shape (`depTimeLocal`, `destination`, `flightNumber`) is still untested against populated data; the existing TODO at `host.js:167-172` documents this and the slice-C/F consumers handle null fields gracefully. Future session: re-validate against a populated capture.

### ORS Sandbox slice 3 (a · b · c) + slice 6d — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel ORS Sandbox results card (`/app/com/scheduling*`) + settings drawer + scenario card. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/sandbox-backtest-store.js` (NEW), `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `manifest.json`.

Three trust+audit slices that close the slice-3 roadmap, plus the slice-6d ergonomics bonus. Together they make Tier 3 Auto-Pricing write-back legible — the user can see whether projections are trustworthy before committing them. 6d ships alongside because it's pure ergonomics (~30 LOC) and the slider reset compounds naturally with the new confidence pill.

**3a — Confidence pill on the Outcome card.** Every projection now shows a small coloured pill above the Outcome table summarising three signals into a high/medium/low level: (1) per-route T-calibration freshness (calibrated <30d → +1, 30–90d → 0, uncalibrated or >90d → −1), (2) ORS data age across cached classes (<7d / 7–30d / >30d), (3) connection-count sample size summed across Y/C/F (≥10 / 5–9 / <5). Aggregate score determines the pill colour; hover for the per-signal tooltip. Pure read of already-loaded data — no fetches, no storage, no model touch. New `_orsSandboxConfidence(route, result)` + `_buildOrsSandboxConfidencePill(confidence)` instance methods next to `_clampedClasses`. ~50 LOC.

**3b — Back-test logging store.** Each Calibrate-T click logs a back-test entry capturing the projection (share / pax / rev / profit + scenario + modelParams) plus the observed share that drove the calibration. Cap 50 entries per route via shift(). Subsequent markets-page bulk scrapes back-fill `observed.share` for entries logged within an 8-day window before the scrape, so calibrations that don't have an immediate observation still gather one when fresh marketShare lands. Tier 3 apply hook can add a second log call with one line — pre-staged via the `trigger: "tier3-apply"` value in the entry shape. New module `modules/route-assistant/sandbox-backtest-store.js` (~150 LOC) mirrors `route-overrides-store.js` pattern: `static PREFIX`, `_pairKey`, `get` / `getMany` / `loadAll` / `log` / `remove` / `backfillManyFromMarketShares` / `_normaliseEntry` / `_normaliseScenario`. Pair key directional. Hooks: `_calibrateOrsSandboxT` re-projects with the new T explicitly so the logged projection matches the calibrated state, then `RouteAssistantSandboxBacktestStore.log(...)`. `_runBulkMarketScrape` calls `RouteAssistantSandboxBacktestStore.backfillManyFromMarketShares(pairs, msByPair, ourEnterpriseId)` after `_applyCachedDemand` — one combined `bulkLoadCache({families: ["marketShare"]})` read so back-fill costs ≈ one extra storage round trip per bulk run. Manifest entry registered between `ors-model.js` and `toast-host.js`.

**3c — Model-fit summary in the settings drawer.** The ORS Sandbox expander gains a new info row above the modelParams inputs: `Model fit: bias +X.Xpp, RMSE Y.Ypp · N routes · M observations · [Refresh]`. Aggregates back-test entries across every route via `RouteAssistantSandboxBacktestStore.loadAll()`; only routes with ≥3 back-filled observations contribute (avoids single-observation noise). Reports bias = mean signed error (positive ⇒ projections under-estimate share) and RMSE = root-mean-square error, both in percentage points. Insufficient-data fallback message when fewer than 3 observations across the whole user. Refresh button reloads + recomputes (cheap — only reads chrome.storage.local). New `_computeOrsSandboxModelFit(allBacktests)` + `_renderOrsSandboxModelFitRow(host)` instance methods near `_clampedClasses`. ~80 LOC.

**6d — Reset-per-slider.** Every Y/C/F price slider, the cargo slider, the frequency input, and the comfort selector now have a small ↺ button next to the readout. Click → restore the route's actual current value (1.0× for price/cargo multipliers, `route.currentFrequency` for freq, 0 for comfort) and dispatch the control's native input/change event so the existing onChange recompute fires. Local helper `mkResetBtn(title, onClick)` defined at the top of `_buildOrsSandboxScenarioCard`. Recovery for "I've explored too far" without leaving the sandbox. ~30 LOC across the four control sites.

### Auto-Pricing Tier 3.1 — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel — Auto-Pricing settings expander · row context menu · ORS Sandbox card · new bulk-apply modal. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/pricing-applier.js` (NEW, ~530 LOC), `modules/route-assistant/pricing-apply-log.js` (NEW, ~245 LOC), extensions to `modules/route-assistant/panel.js` (~1100 LOC) and `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js` (`pricing.apply` block + `_mergePricing` deep-merge), `manifest.json`.

The first slice of Auto-Pricing **write-back** to AS. Slice 3.1 is **dry-run only** — the full pipeline (GET handshake → preflight → form-body construction → audit log) runs on every Apply, but no POST hits AS. Tier 3.2 (next session) flips one default to enable real writes; 3.3 adds bulk Apply + silent-auto.

**Form target.** `POST /app/com/markets/<HUB><DEST>?<wicket>-1.-pair-pair~panel-settings-settings~form` with body fields `classes:prices:0:newPrice` (Y), `:1:` (C), `:2:` (F), `:3:` (Cargo), the five General Settings selects (round-tripped at their current values on price-only applies), the four scope checkboxes (`settings:airportPair` / `settings:flightNumbers` / `settings:returnAirportPair` / `settings:returnFlightNumbers`), and the disambiguating submit-button name (default `submit-prices`). Mirrors `ors-scraper.js`'s wicket handshake exactly — fresh GET per Apply (no session reuse) so PageExpiredException can't silently corrupt.

**Two-gate model.** Real writes need BOTH `settings.routeAssistant.pricing.apply.enabled = true` AND `apply.dryRunOnly = false`. Slice 3.1 ships `apply.enabled = false` AND `apply.dryRunOnly = true` — flipping just one doesn't unlock anything. Belt-and-braces: each gate has a separate semantic meaning in 3.2+ (kill switch vs slice gate). The applier instance constructor enforces both — even if a caller passes `{dryRun: false}`, the instance's `dryRunOnly` overrides.

**`RouteAssistantPricingApplier`** (pricing-applier.js).
- `apply(hub, dest, prices, opts)` — async; orchestrates GET → `parseFormContext` → `preflight` → `buildBody` → POST (skipped in dry-run) → response parse + verify. Returns `{status: "dry-run"|"verified"|"posted"|"failed"|"aborted", logId, prevPrices, newPrices, preflight, bodyPreview, ...}`.
- `static parseFormContext(html)` — extracts wicket session, form action path, hidden inputs, current Y/C/F/Cargo prices, defaults, slider ranges (per-class min/max from inline `slider({…})` script regex), and General Settings select values. Returns null when the pricing form can't be located (page format change, not logged in). Trusts the snapshot's `name=` attribute over the static `FIELD_NAMES` map so an AS reorder of the table doesn't misalign indices.
- `static buildBody({formContext, prices, settings, scope, submitButton})` — assembles `URLSearchParams`. Missing classes round-trip at their current value (partial-update safe). Submit button name included as a body field per Wicket convention.
- `static preflight({formContext, prices, warnAboveDeltaPct, cooldownMinPerRoute, lastApplyAt})` — pure-function; returns `{blockers, warnings, deltas, percentDeltas}`. Blockers (disable Apply): `noFormContext`, `noPrices`, `negativePrice`, `outOfSliderRange`, `cooldownActive`. Warnings (informational): `largeDelta` (≥ `warnAboveDeltaPct`).
- `static fingerprint(hub, dest, prices, scope)` — deterministic hash for idempotency dedup (5-min window) by the apply log.
- `_verify(hub, dest)` — re-fetch + parse `currentPrices` for post-write verification (Tier 3.2 entry; ready in 3.1).

**`RouteAssistantPricingApplyLog`** (pricing-apply-log.js).
- Two stores, both written atomically per apply: global timeline `routeAssistant:pricingApplyLog` (cap 200, dedup-merged on identical fingerprint within 5 min) + per-route ring `routeAssistant:pricingApplyLog:<HUB>-<DEST>` (cap 20, no dedup).
- Records: `{id, ts, hub, dest, status, source, scope, prevPrices, newPrices, requestedPrices, verifiedPrices, verifyAt, httpStatus, fingerprint, reason, sandboxScenario, projectedDelta, preflight: {blockers, warnings, deltas, percentDeltas}, error, warning, bodyPreview, dryRun, count}`. Auto-cleanups: function-references stripped, oversized strings truncated.
- API: `add(record, {dedupWindowMs})`, `update(id, patch)`, `getRecent(n)`, `getForRoute(hub, dest, n)`, `getLastSuccessAt(hub, dest)` (for cooldown), `getLastSuccessMap(pairs)` (bulk), `prune(newLimit)`, `clear()`.

**Three apply UX entry points.**
- **Row context menu (primary single-route path)** — third item "Apply price…" added next to the existing override editor + ORS Sandbox drill-in. Opens the per-route preview modal pre-filled with cached `ownPricing.prices`.
- **Sandbox card "Apply this scenario as price…"** — bridge from the simulation card to the write modal. Reads the resolved per-class scenario prices from `_orsSandboxResult.perClass.<cls>.scenarioPrice` (fallback: `current × multiplier`) and seeds the modal inputs. Carries the projected delta envelope (paxPerWeek, profitPerWeek, share, rating) into the apply log so a future back-test can join projected vs observed.
- **Bulk apply modal (3.3 entry point, 3.1 lists routes only)** — opens from the Auto-Pricing expander's "Open bulk apply…" CTA. Table of every visible route with cached pricing; per-row "Open…" button opens the single-route modal. 3.1 lists routes only; 3.3 turns this into a multi-row preview with Apply across selection.

**Per-route apply modal** (`_openPricingApplyModal`). Per-class price grid (Y/C/F/Cargo) with current value + live Δ% colored by `warnAboveDeltaPct` / `requireConfirmAboveDeltaPct` thresholds; scope checkboxes (4); optional reason textarea (≤ 240 chars, persisted to log); sandbox-projection mini-card when launched from the sandbox; collapsible "What gets posted" body preview; preflight host that fills after Dry-run preview with blockers/warnings or "preflight passed". Two action buttons — Dry-run preview (always enabled) + Apply (disabled in 3.1; tooltip explains the gate). Toast on result.

**Auto-Pricing expander Tier 3 sub-section** (`_renderTier3ApplyBlock`). Purple-tinted block under the rose Live-route-data block. Header shows current stage badge (3.1 = amber dry-run, 3.2-disabled = grey, 3.2-enabled = green). Surfaces: rationale text · Apply-enabled toggle · default-scope row (4 checkboxes) · "Open bulk apply…" CTA · live "Recent applies" preview list (last 10 entries, click a row → `_scrollToRouteRow` to that route). Preview re-fetches after every apply via `_refreshAllOpenTier3LogPreviews` so users see entries appear in real time.

**Settings.** New `pricing.apply` sub-block: `enabled` (kill switch), `dryRunOnly` (slice gate), `defaultScope: {airportPair, flightNumbers, returnAirportPair, returnFlightNumbers}`, `roundPolicy`, `cooldownMinPerRoute` (default 60), `warnAboveDeltaPct` (5), `requireConfirmAboveDeltaPct` (15), `pricingApplyLogLimit` (200), `perRouteApplyLogLimit` (20), `submitButton`, `showRecentApplies`, `recentApplyPreviewCount`. Pre-staged for 3.3: `silentAutoMaxPerDay/Hour`, `silentAutoMinDeltaPct`. New `_mergePricing` deep-merge so partial saves don't blow away `defaultScope` siblings.

**Verification against real HTML.** Sample `/Users/jihwan/Desktop/CLAUDE/https-:free1.airlinesim.aero:app:com:markets:JFKATL?869.html` confirmed: wicket session `869`, form action path ends `…-pair-pair~panel-settings-settings~form`, all 4 price input names match (`classes:prices:0..3:newPrice`), all 3 submit buttons present (`p::submit` / `submit-prices` / `submit-settings`), 4 slider ranges parsed from inline scripts (`1..296` / `1..640` / `1..1130` / `1..214` for Y/C/F/Cargo), hidden-fields div empty (matches the ORS form pattern).

**3.2 next.** Flip `apply.dryRunOnly` default to false; modal's Apply button activates when both gates clear; receipt verify post-POST; `_undoableSave` integration so a misclicked write is one toast click away from being reverted (re-applies prevPrices via the same applier path); per-route + global cooldown enforced from `apply-log.getLastSuccessAt`; rate-limit/circuit-breaker on consecutive 429/503 mirrors the ORS pattern.

**3.3 next.** Bulk apply modal turns into a selection table with Apply across N routes; silent-auto loop behind `silentAutoEnabled` with hard caps (`silentAutoMaxPerDay`, `silentAutoMaxPerHour`, `silentAutoMinDeltaPct`); confirmation modal on first activation.

### ORS Sandbox slice 2c — shipped a prior session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel ORS Sandbox card (`/app/com/scheduling*`) + settings drawer. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/rating-observation-store.js` (NEW), `modules/route-assistant/rating-alpha-store.js` (NEW), `modules/route-assistant/ors-scraper.js`, `modules/route-assistant/demand-derivator.js`, `modules/route-assistant/ors-model.js`, `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `manifest.json`.

Replaces the global `α_price = 8` (rating points lost per +100% price change) with **a per-route, per-class value derived from observations logged on every ORS scrape**, plus a manual override slot. Closes the last open slice-2 item; ORS Sandbox slice 2 is now fully shipped.

**Observation log.** New `routeAssistant:ratingObservations:<HUB>-<DEST>` directional store. After every `RouteAssistantOrsScraper.scrape()` saves (line 616), `_logRatingObservation` reads the matching markets-page `ownPricing` snapshot and writes `{at, prices:{Y,C,F}, ratings:{Y,C,F}, ownConnections:{Y,C,F}, competitorConnections:{Y,C,F}, comfortLevel, pricingScrapedAt, orsScrapedAt}`. Inside the scraper (NOT the panel) so bulk syncs log too — `bulkScrape` at `ors-scraper.js:809` loops calling `scrape()`. Append-only with FIFO cap 50 / 90d age prune on every read; honors `settings.routeAssistant.orsSandbox.ratingObservations.autoLogOnScrape` (default true, stable across versions).

**Regression.** `RouteAssistantDemandDerivator.derive(...)` extended to accept an observations record. New `_ratingPriceElasticity(observations, cls, notes, opts)` runs per class with this filter pipeline: (1) drop pairs where price/rating non-finite or non-positive, (2) drop where pricing/ORS scrape gap > 24h, (3) drop where comfortLevel changed vs prior obs, (4) drop where ownConnections delta ≥ 1 (own-frequency confound), (5) drop where competitor connection count jumped > 20%, (6) need ≥ minObs (default 4), (7) need range(priceDev%) ≥ 8% lever arm, (8) need ≥ 2 distinct priceDev% buckets at 1% rounding, (9) OLS via existing `_linregSlope`, (10) negate ONCE → magnitude, (11) reject if magnitude < 0 (non-monotone), (12) clip to `[0, 50]`. Backwards-compat: legacy 4-arg call `derive(h, i, o, opts)` still works (heuristic detects `opts` shape vs observation record shape).

**Cascade resolver.** Per-route per-class α resolution order in `_recomputeOrsSandbox`: **override > derived (≥minObs) > siblingDerived > fleetMedian > global default**. Sibling cascade only fires from classes whose source is already "derived" on this route (one hop, no cascade-chains). Fleet-median computed in `RouteAssistantPanel._computeFleetMedianAlpha(rows)` once per `_applyCachedDemand` pass; skipped when fewer than 5 routes contributed. Two-pass resolver in `_recomputeOrsSandbox`: first pass resolves override/derived; second pass cascades unresolved classes.

**Sign convention.** α stored everywhere as a **positive magnitude** in `[0, 50]`. The OLS slope is naturally negative on well-behaved routes; negated ONCE inside `_ratingPriceElasticity`. The model formula at `ors-model.js:466` keeps its existing `base − α × priceRatio` shape (no `Math.abs` at the consumer). Panel reads with `Number.isFinite`, never `||`, so a manual override of `0` (route's rating doesn't respond to price for that class) is honored verbatim.

**UI.** New collapsed `<details>` expander INSIDE the Outcome card via `_buildOrsSandboxAlphaExpander(result, route)` — three rows (Y/C/F), each shows the resolved α + source label and lets the user pin a manual value. Save wraps in `_undoableSave` so a misclick is one click of Undo away. Settings drawer's `_renderOrsSandboxSection` gains an "Auto-log rating observations on every ORS scrape" toggle + total observation count + "Reset all observations" button (destructive, confirms before wiping). Model emits per-class source notes ("class Y: α=6.2 (derived from 12 observations)" etc.) into the existing notes footer.

**Why slice 2c, why now.** Tier 3 Auto-Pricing write-back depends on accurate share/profit projections; slice 2 closed three of four model-precision gaps but α stayed global. Slice 2c lets the model self-tune per route as the user runs ORS scrapes during normal play — gated by data accumulation, not session work. Cold-start UX kept sensible by the cascade fallback; no surprise behaviour change on day one.

### ORS Sandbox slice 2 (a · b · d · e) — shipped a prior session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel ORS Sandbox card (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/ors-model.js`, `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`.

Four model-precision slices, all from the slice-2 roadmap. Slice 2c shipped this session — see directly above.

**2b — Independent per-class price control.** The single Y-anchored `priceMultiplier` was replaced with `priceMultipliers: {Y, C, F}`. Three sliders render in the scenario card (one per cabin class with a cached observed fare); routes with only Y cached collapse back to a single slider — visually identical to slice 1. The C/F preview row is gone (sliders show new prices directly). New `RouteAssistantOrsModel._normaliseScenario` is the canonicaliser: accepts both the slice 1 shape (single `priceMultiplier`) and the slice 2 shape, always returns the slice 2 shape. The pax-pool elasticity now reads the **Y** multiplier only — pax pool is Y-anchored per the demand-derivator's series. Saved scenarios under the legacy shape auto-migrate at `_mergeOrsSandbox` read time via the new `_coerceScenarioMultipliers` helper that clamps each class to `[0.30, 3.00]`.

**2a — Frequency synthesis.** When `newFreq > currentFreq`, the model now synthesises additional own-connection rows before per-class ranking instead of greying out rank/share. Implementation: `_synthesizeOwnConnections(classRec, baseFreq, requestedExtras, notes, cls)` clones a template (preferring own + nonstop, else highest-rated own); each clone keeps the template's rating, totalDuration, totalPrice, sets `bookable: true`, deep-clones legs with `isOurs: true` forced, distinct synthetic flightCode (`synth-<cls>-<i>`). **No timestamps** are needed — connection schemas in cache have none and the model's rank/softmax doesn't read them. Saturation is capped at +ceil(0.5 × baseFreq) via `_capSynthesisCount` so adding 30 synth rows to a 5-row list can't push share to ~1.0 in a way the user can't sanity-check; the cap also respects `MAX_FOR_SOFTMAX`. `newFreq < baseFreq` keeps the slice-1 LF-dilute behaviour (you can't tell which connections to drop without timestamps); a note surfaces this asymmetry. Notes also call out synth count, saturation cap, and "no own connection to clone" skip.

**2d — Cargo branch.** `perClass.CARGO` is now populated when `route.orsByClass.CARGO.connections[]` is cached, alongside Y/C/F. New `_projectCargo(arg)` mirrors `_projectClass` but simpler — no comfort lift, no rating shift (the `cargoMultiplier` scales yield only). A new cargo slider appears in the scenario card when CARGO is cached; it scales `cargoYieldPerKgKm` via the existing profit-estimator `override.cargoYieldPerKgKm` hook (no estimator changes needed — verified at `profit-estimator.js:200` where `effectiveCargoYield × cargoLoadFactor × distance` folds into revenue). The cargo demand pool also shifts via `cargoElasticity` when `cargoMultiplier !== 1` so `useRealDemandForLF` users see demand drop proportionally. Output envelope adds `baseline.cargoPerWeek` / `projected.cargoPerWeek` / `delta.cargoPerWeek` (separate from `paxPerWeek` so pax aggregates stay clean); the results card renders a Cargo/wk row when present. `_assembleOrsSandboxRoute` now propagates `cargoElasticity` from the demand-derivator output. Notes: "cargo multiplier scales yield only — no rating shift modelled" when `cargoMultiplier !== 1`; "no CARGO connection list cached — cargo profit unchanged by share" when only the pool is cached.

**2e — Visual rating-clamp annotation.** The Outcome card's `renderRow` now accepts an `annotation` argument; the Rating row passes one built by `_clampedClasses(result)` that scans `result.perClass.<cls>.priceRatio` (matches the model's `RATING_CLAMP_LOW/HIGH = 0.5/1.5`). When any class clamped, the projected Rating cell shows a `*` and an amber colour with a tooltip listing which classes hit the floor/ceiling — no more reading the notes footer to know that a rating projection was clipped.

### ORS Sandbox slice 1.5 — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel ORS Sandbox card (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`.

Two correctness/ergonomics fixes on top of slice 1 — small but they close gaps the user would have hit on their second route.

**Per-route scenario state.** Slice 1's `lastScenario` was a single global record — switching from JFK→LAX to JFK→ORD inherited the prior route's slider positions (almost always wrong). Replaced with `lastScenarioByRoute: {[hub-dest]: scenario}`. The `_mergeOrsSandbox` deep-merge validates each entry's shape (numeric `priceMultiplier`, numeric-or-null `frequency`, numeric `comfortDelta`) and silently drops malformed records. **One-shot legacy migration:** when settings load and a stored config has a global `lastScenario` plus `lastRouteIata`, settings-store surfaces it as `_legacyLastScenario` (a transient field, never re-persisted); the panel reads it as a fallback exactly when the user reopens the route they had open pre-upgrade. First save through `_recomputeOrsSandbox` writes per-route and `delete`s both legacy keys, so the migration completes silently on the first slider drag.

**Calibration freshness.** Markets drift; a 30-day-old T is worth re-running. `perRouteTemperatureCalibratedAt: {[hub-dest]: ms}` sibling map captures the timestamp on every Calibrate-T click. The sandbox's T banner now reads "Using calibrated T = 18.4 for this route · global T = 25 · calibrated 12d ago [reset]" with the age coloured grey (<30d), amber (≥30d, "consider recalibrating"), or red (≥90d, "recalibrate"). The settings drawer status line shows "(N stale ≥30d)" so users see calibration decay across all routes without opening each one. Reset-all and per-route reset both clear the timestamp alongside the T.

**Why it's slice 1.5 not slice 2:** these fix slice 1's stated UX limitations (per-route scenario was implicit-but-wrong; calibration drift was silent). Slice 2 is the model precision work (cargo, frequency synthesis, independent class pricing) — see "ORS Sandbox roadmap" below.

### Polish bundle — shipped this session
**Surfaces across:** route-assistant panel + multi-class ORS settings + helpers. **Files:** `.gitignore`, `helpers.js`, `modules/route-assistant/{panel,settings-store,inventory-page-scraper,ors-scraper}.js`.

Cross-cutting fixes after a beta-readiness audit. None change behaviour visibly to a normal user; all close silent footguns.

| Fix | File | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Scratch HTML/txt fixtures gitignored | `.gitignore` | Five untracked dev-reference files (`USA Stations.html`, `created station after.html`, `html - for smaller countries without reigions.html`, `station alabama.html`, `aircraft.txt`) would have packaged into the .crx (~218 KB plus a leaked spec note). |
| `escapeHtml` hoisted to helpers.js | `helpers.js`, `panel.js` | `wave-overlay.js` (manifest line 133) calls `escapeHtml` but it lived in `panel.js:8087` (loads after). Worked at runtime only because methods aren't called at parse time. Helpers.js is in the first content-script block — every page guarantees it's available. |
| `_mergeOrs` renormalises classWeights | `settings-store.js:_mergeOrs` | A stored partial `{ECONOMY: 1.00}` merged with defaults `{0.75, 0.20, 0.05}` summed to 1.25; composite ORS scorer didn't renormalise. New `_renormaliseWeights` helper clamps non-negative entries and divides through sum. |
| `_diffConfig` tolerant of reordered set arrays | `panel.js:_diffConfig` | Export-import roundtrip flagged `classesToScrape: ["ECONOMY","BUSINESS","FIRST"]` vs `["FIRST","BUSINESS","ECONOMY"]` as changed. Added `_DIFF_SET_PATH_KEYS` whitelist (`classesToScrape` only currently) + `_arrayEqualsAsSet` helper; ordered arrays still diff strictly via `_arraysEqual`. |
| Legacy `defaultPayload` dropped on save | `settings-store.js:_mergeOrs` | Lazy-rename to `primaryClass` (slice-2-multi-class shape) was reading the legacy field but never deleting it; round-tripped forever. `delete out.defaultPayload` after the rename. |
| `parseIntSafe` decimal-aware | `inventory-page-scraper.js` | Old impl stripped `.` and `,` indiscriminately — `"$1,234.56"` → 123456 (100× too big). Latent (`avgFare` not consumed downstream) but a footgun. New impl detects the rightmost separator with a 1–2 digit tail as a decimal point; thousand separators stripped. 12/12 smoke-tests pass. |
| Route-note popover unsaved-state UX | `panel.js:_openRouteNotePopover` | Cancel/outside-click/Escape now confirms before discarding unsaved typing. Save is disabled while textarea is empty (only Delete can remove an existing note). Save with unchanged text early-returns instead of writing a fresh `updatedAt`. |
| Restored ors-scraper comments | `ors-scraper.js` | Two helpful inline comments (`PageExpiredException` watch + `safetyCap` rationale) were dropped during the multi-class refactor. Restored. |

### Q4 saved table views + Q6 retirement planner + Q10 opening checklist — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** controls bar (Q4 view dropdown), 🛩 panel-header button (Q6 modal), right-click on NEW-status rows (Q10 popover). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`.

Operational planning slice — three independent items shipped together. All read-only against AS; Q4 is the foundation primitive.

**Q4 saved table views.** New `settings.routeAssistant.savedViews: []`. Each entry: `{id, name, filters, viewMode, sortField, sortDir, compactView, createdAt}`. Quick-switch dropdown + Save-current button (`+`) + Delete prompt (`🗑`) rendered in the controls bar via `_renderSavedViewsControl(host)`. Selecting a view writes its snapshot back into settings (deep-merge for `filters.statuses` so a save predating a new status type still loads cleanly) and re-renders. Save-current snapshots the current state via `window.prompt` for the name. Delete via numeric prompt; toast comes with one-click Undo via the existing toast `action` slot.
- **Snapshot covers**: filters block, view mode (pax/cargo/all), sort field + direction, compact view.
- **Snapshot does NOT cover**: search query, Q1 quick-filter chip toggles, watchlist starring. Intentionally ephemeral — saving them would surprise users when applying an old view.
- **Composes with future column-customization slices**: when U7 / U8 ship, `columnOrder` and `columnVisibility` extend the snapshot shape and the existing apply path picks them up automatically.

**Q6 aircraft retirement planner.** New 🛩 button in panel header opens `_openRetirementPlanner()`. Modal lists every owned aircraft sorted by age desc with retirement timeline. `MAX_LIFE_YEARS = 25` (per AS rules). Header strip with a numeric "highlight aircraft retiring within N months" input (default 24); summary chip shows count within threshold. Per-row table:
- Reg. · Equipment · Age (yr) · Until retire (yr) · Status · "Find replacement →" link
- Rows past life: red background + ⌛ pill
- Rows within threshold: yellow background + ⏳ pill
- "Find replacement" opens the dashboard's Used Aircraft Scanner in a new tab.

Reads from `this.fleet.aircraft` (already populated by `RouteAssistantFleetStore`). No new I/O.

**Q10 route opening checklist.** New right-click menu entry "Opening checklist…" rendered conditionally when `row.status === "NEW"`. Opens `_openRouteOpeningChecklist(row)` modal with 7 prerequisites:
1. Distance resolved (from `row.distanceKm`)
2. AS demand seeded (from `row.paxScore`)
3. Aircraft selected (from `_fleetContext()`)
4. Service profile set (from `serviceConfigMap`)
5. Markets scraped (from `row.ownPricing` / `row.marketSharePeriod`)
6. ORS connection list cached (from `row.orsByClass`)
7. Carriers list cached (from `row.carriers`)

Each item shows ✓/✗ + a one-line hint + a "fix this →" button when ✗ that opens the relevant AS page or fires a toast pointing at the right Settings expander. Header summary: "X / 7 ready". Pure derivations from existing row decorations + cached records — no fresh I/O, no new storage keys.

### Q3 override expiration dates — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** override editor + Dest cell. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/route-overrides-store.js` (extended), `modules/route-assistant/panel.js` (extended), `modules/route-assistant/aggregator.js` (extended).

"Test this LF for 4 weeks then revert" — overrides now carry an optional `expiresAt` unix-ms timestamp. The full override editor gains an "Expires in N days" input (empty = never expires, default behaviour preserved). Once expired, the record stays in storage with a ⌛ marker on the Dest cell so the user can see + clear it; the aggregator passes `null` to the estimator so $/flt/$/wk reflect the demand-driven defaults.

**Store extension (`route-overrides-store.js`):**
- `_clean` accepts optional numeric `expiresAt` (any positive timestamp; past values are valid — the store doesn't reject them since callers handle expiry).
- `_hasAnyValue` extended so a save with only `expiresAt` doesn't accidentally collapse to a `remove()`.
- New static helpers: `isExpired(record, nowMs)` (true when `expiresAt ≤ now`) and `daysUntilExpiry(record, nowMs)` (negative = already expired, in days).
- File header documents the new field; key shape gains the optional `expiresAt`.

**Override editor (`_openOverrideEditor`):**
- New "Expires in" row below "Note" — number input (0–3650 days) + live-updating hint text. Empty = "days · empty = never expires"; positive value = "expires <localized date>"; pre-filled with current days-remaining when an unexpired `expiresAt` is in the record (or the localized expiry date when already expired, in red).
- Save translates the days input into `expiresAt = Date.now() + days × 86400000`. Empty / 0 / non-numeric → null (clears expiry). Editing an existing override RESETS the clock at save time — the user re-confirms intent.

**Cross-save preservation:** every other save path now carries `expiresAt` through unchanged so the user's TTL doesn't get wiped on incidental yield/LF tweaks elsewhere:
- Profit-modifier popover Save: passes `expiresAt: existing.expiresAt` (the popover doesn't expose an expiry input).
- Profit-modifier popover Clear → Undo restore: re-saves prev with prev's `expiresAt`.
- Q2 bulk override `_applyBulkOverride.mergeRecord`: prev's `expiresAt` carried through; bulk modal doesn't expose an expiry input either (would clutter the bulk UI).
- Q2 Undo restore: prev's `expiresAt` re-saved.

**Aggregator skip-applying:**
- The estimator call site at `aggregator.js:259` now gates the `override` parameter through `RouteAssistantRouteOverridesStore.isExpired(row.override) ? null : row.override`. The estimator falls back to demand-driven LF / configured base yield when expired — exactly the same path it would take if no override existed.
- The override RECORD remains attached to `row.override` so the UI can render the ⌛ marker and the editor can still display the expired values for review.

**Row decoration in `_renderRows`:**
- After `_detectStatusTransitions`, a new walker decorates every row with `_expired` (boolean), `_expiresSoon` (boolean, true when 0 < days ≤ 7), and `_expiresDays` (number, can be negative). Pure derivation from `row.override.expiresAt` against `Date.now()`.

**Visual marker** in the Dest cell render closure:
- Existing 📌 pin stays (route has any override).
- ⌛ glyph (grey, "Override expired Nd ago — falling back to defaults") appended when `row._expired`.
- ⏳ glyph (yellow, "Override expires in N days …") appended when `row._expiresSoon`. Tooltip nudges the user to right-click to renew or clear.
- No new icon when override is fresh (no expiry, or expiry > 7 days away) — keeps the cell clean for the common case.

**Limits:**
- Q9 bulk frequency template (also blocked on Tier 3 write-back) doesn't have any UI for setting expiry — same reason as Q2: the bulk modal is too cluttered. Per-route editor is the only place to set/edit expiry.
- The `expiresSoon` threshold is hardcoded at 7 days. If users want a different threshold the natural place is `settings.routeAssistant.overrides.expiresSoonDays` — defer to a follow-on slice if requested.
- Already-expired records aren't auto-deleted. They stay in storage until the user explicitly clears via the override editor's "Clear" button or via Q2 with all fields ticked + applied (which writes a fresh record). Auto-cleanup-on-mount is a follow-on if the user reports storage growth.

### Q8 status-change history per route — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** St cell tooltip on every row of the Route Assistant table. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/status-history-store.js` (NEW), `modules/route-assistant/panel.js` (extended), `manifest.json`.

Operational memory for long-running games. Was this route always OVER, or did it pop into OVER yesterday after a frequency increase? Hover the St cell and the answer is right there: `OVER since 12d · was OK before · 4 transitions tracked`.

**Detection.** A new `_detectStatusTransitions(rows)` step in `_renderRows` (after scoring + watchlist + alert evaluation, before sort) walks every scored row and:
1. Looks up the route's history in `this.statusHistoryMap`.
2. If the latest entry's `to` matches `row.status`, no-op (dedup).
3. Otherwise builds a new transition `{from: <last.to | null>, to: row.status, at: Date.now()}` and:
   - Updates the in-memory map immediately so the St cell tooltip reflects the new transition without waiting for the storage round-trip.
   - Fires `RouteAssistantStatusHistoryStore.appendTransition(...)` as fire-and-forget.
4. Decorates `row._statusHistory = record` regardless of whether a new transition was appended this render. The St render closure reads `row._statusHistory` directly — keeps the closure free of `this`-dependence.

**Store** at `routeAssistant:statusHistory:<HUB>-<DEST>` → `{hub, dest, transitions: [{from, to, at}, …], updatedAt}`. Append-only with capacity capped at `MAX_TRANSITIONS = 20` per route — so a long-running game doesn't accumulate unbounded storage. Pair key is **directional** (matches override/note stores). Static API: `get / getMany / appendTransition / latestTransition / remove`. CRUD pattern mirrors `route-note-store.js`.

**St cell tooltip extension.** The status column's render closure (originally just `td.title = "${status}: ${description}"`) now composes a multi-line tooltip:
```
OVER: route flying over capacity vs scored seats
OVER since 12d · was OK before
4 transitions tracked
Recent: OK→OVER (12d), UNDER→OK (35d), NEW→UNDER (61d)
```
Uses the existing `_formatRelativeTime` helper with the `" ago"` suffix stripped. Maxes the recent-history footer at the 5 newest entries.

**Bulk-load on refresh.** `refresh()` calls `RouteAssistantStatusHistoryStore.getMany(pairs)` once for every visible destIata; the result lands in `this.statusHistoryMap` keyed by `<HUB>-<DEST>` uppercased. Renders read from the in-memory map — no per-render storage round-trip.

**Limits / out of scope:**
- The first-ever observation of a route appends `{from: null, to: <status>, at: now}` — the tooltip displays this as "first observed" since there's no prior status. This means every route starts accumulating history on first sight.
- Status-history storage growth is bounded at 20 transitions × every route × every hub. At scale (50 hubs × 200 routes per hub × 20 transitions × ~40 bytes) ≈ 8 MB, well within `unlimitedStorage` permission.
- No alert-trigger integration yet — Q+ alert rules don't read `_statusHistory` (they check `_diff` only). Adding "alert me when status changes to OVER" is a follow-on slice.

### Cluster A — Bulk operations (U5 + U12 + Q2 + Q5) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant table (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`.

Foundation primitive (multi-select) + 3 follow-on bulk workflows. Read-only against AS — every mutation routes through `_undoableSave` so any bulk write is one click of Undo away.

**U5 multi-select rows.** Constructor adds `_selectedRoutes = new Set()` (destIata uppercased) + `_selectAnchorDest` (for shift+click range). Built inline in `_buildTable`:
- New checkbox column at index 0 (NOT in `RouteAssistantPanel.COLUMNS` — schema for sort/group/scoring/freeze stays clean).
- Header has a select-all checkbox with three states (checked / indeterminate / unchecked) recomputed each render against the visible row set.
- Body checkboxes wired to `_handleRowSelectClick(row, ev)`: plain click toggles + sets anchor; shift+click ranges from anchor to clicked in current visible order; ⌘/ctrl+click = plain (checkboxes already discrete).
- Selection survives sort changes (anchor + set are tracked by destIata).
- Filter changes drop hidden routes via the intersection at end of `_renderRows`; fires a 4-second info toast `"3 routes hidden by filter — selection updated"` when ≥1 drops.
- Selected rows get a 3px purple left-edge band (`border-left: 3px solid #a78bfa`).
- Right-click menu (`_openRowContextMenu`) gains a conditional `"Add <DEST> to selection (N)"` / `"Remove <DEST> from selection (N)"` entry when ≥1 selected.

**U12 selection footer.** `_renderSelectionFooter(visibleRows)` mounted into `tableHost` after `_buildTable` runs. Sticky-bottom (`position: sticky; bottom: 0; z-index: 3`) so it pins to the visible edge during long-table scroll. Skipped entirely in Wave View / ORS Sandbox / heatmap modes (selection state is preserved in memory, footer just doesn't render). Layout: count chip · Σprofit/wk · avg score · avg ourPaxShare · Edit-overrides button · Compare button (enabled only at exactly 2 selected) · Clear button. All metrics reduce from `_selectedRoutes ∩ scoredRows` in one pass.

**Q2 bulk override editor.** `_openBulkOverrideEditor(selectedRows)` opens a modal with: chip list of selected routes (× per-chip removes from selection) + 4 input rows. Each input row is `[apply-this-checkbox] [label] [number-input]` — the input is disabled + muted when "apply this" is off, so users can't accidentally write a value they didn't intend to apply. Placeholder text: blank (no override), the value (all routes agree), or `"varies"` (routes have different existing values). Confirm dialog above N=20; progress toast above N=50.
- Apply path goes through `_applyBulkOverride(selectedRows, fieldsToApply)`:
  1. Capture every prev override into `prevByPair` before any save.
  2. For each selected route, build merged record = prev fields preserved EXCEPT for the user's checked fields. Critical: `RouteOverridesStore.save` is full-replace (route-overrides-store.js:80–87), so we MUST merge with prev or we lose untouched fields per route.
  3. `Promise.all(routes.map(...))` parallel writes — ~10× wall-time savings at N=50.
  4. Update `overrideMap` + `row.override` per-route.
  5. Single `_recomputeProfit()` at the end (not per-route — major perf win).
  6. Wrap in `_undoableSave` with batched restore that re-saves prev records in parallel (or `remove(...)` when prev was null).

**Q5 compare two routes modal.** `_openCompareModal(selectedRows)` triggered from the U12 footer's Compare button (visible only when `_selectedRoutes.size === 2`). 3-column table (route A · route B · Δ) for ~15 fields. Direction-aware Δ coloring: green when A's value moves the score in the better direction relative to B (per-field `dir: "higher" | "lower" | null`); red opposite; neutral grey when one side is null OR direction is undefined. `"—"` for missing fields with a one-line legend at the top. Per-route Open-in-AS buttons in footer.

### Cluster B — Decision-support overlays (U11 + per-hub topRoutes + Q13) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant table hover (U11) + new 🗺 panel mode (Q13). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`.

**U11 hover row preview card.** Singleton floating card reused across rows. `mouseenter` schedules a 200ms dwell via `_scheduleRowPreview(row, anchorEl)`; mouseleave starts a 150ms grace dismiss via `_scheduleRowPreviewDismiss()`. Cursor entering the card itself cancels the dismiss timer so users can hover the card to copy values.
- Card contents: title (HUB→DEST + city) · status pills (★ starred / 🛠 override / 📝 note / status / aircraft fit) · per-flight breakdown table (pax revenue · cargo revenue · fuel · crew · maintenance · other fixed · profit/flight · profit/week, color-coded green/red on profit lines) · key stats line (score · pool · RM% · ORS gap · share %) · footer (distance · weekly flights · competitors) · inline route note when present.
- Position: prefer right of row; fall back to left; below if neither fits. Viewport-clamped vertically so the card never escapes.

**Per-hub topRoutes refactor (foundation for Q13).** `_publishTopRoutes(visible)` now writes TWO keys per render:
- `routeAssistant:topRoutes` — single global key, current hub. **Legacy contract preserved** so the Used Aircraft Scanner consumer (which reads this for J slice 4 route-fit) keeps working unchanged.
- `routeAssistant:topRoutes:<HUB>` — NEW per-hub key. Each panel mount writes its own hub's row here. Q13 reads from all of them keyed by `recentHubs` to build the matrix.
Slim record now also includes `profitPerWeek` + `ourPaxShare` (used as Q13 metric options alongside score). New static method `RouteAssistantPanel.loadAllHubTopRoutes(hubs)` returns `Map<HUB, blob>` for the heatmap renderer.

**Q13 yield heatmap matrix view.** New 🗺 button in panel header next to 🧪 Sandbox. `settings.routeAssistant.heatmap.{enabled, metric: "score" | "profit" | "share"}`. `_toggleHeatmap` persists; `_renderHeatmap(sorted)` branches in `_renderRows` after Wave View + ORS Sandbox checks (mutual exclusion — wave wins, sandbox second, heatmap third).
- Y axis: current hub first + every other recent hub (deduped).
- X axis: union of all hubs' top-50 destinations, sorted by current hub's score desc.
- Cells: colored chip showing the user-selected metric. Color scale spans the full visible range — three-stop gradient (deep blue → muted amber → vibrant green) via `_heatmapColor(t)`. Click any cell → opens `/app/com/scheduling/<HUB><DEST>` in a new tab.
- Empty cells (`·` in muted color) when a destination isn't in that hub's cached topRoutes (visit the hub once to populate).
- Sticky first column (hub label) + sticky header row (dest IATAs) so the grid stays navigable on wide hub-rich data.
- Header strip: title + "<N> recent hubs" subtitle + Metric selector (Score / Profit / week / Pax share %).
- Empty-state banner when fewer than 2 hubs visited or no cached topRoutes records exist.

### N2 Notification center + Q1 chip bar + Q15 hub quick-swap + progress-toast propagation — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel header (🔔 button), controls bar (chips + hub strip), corner toasts during bulk syncs. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/toast-host.js` (extended), `modules/route-assistant/panel.js` (extended), `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js` (pre-staged by user this session).

A coherent foundation cluster on top of the toast primitive. Each item independently shippable; together they turn "powerful but dense" into "fast to operate."

**Q1 — Quick-filter chips bar.** Pill toggles for content-shape filters: ★ Watch, 💸 Loss, 🛠 Override, 📝 Note, Δ Changed. Settings pre-staged in `filters.{watchlistOnly, lossMakers, hasOverride, hasNote, onlyChanged}`. `_renderQuickFilterChips(host)` renders the strip into the controls bar after the search input. Each chip flips its setting field, persists, and re-renders. Active chips fill with their accent color (gold / red / purple / blue / green); inactive are outline-only. Composes with existing filters (status, minScore, fleetFlyableOnly, search).

**Q15 — Hub keyboard quick-swap.** `settings.recentHubs` (capped at 5, deduped case-insensitively, most-recent-first) tracks every hub the panel has mounted on. `_trackRecentHub(iata)` called from `refresh()` pushes the current hub. `_renderRecentHubsBar(host)` renders chips in the controls bar — each chip shows `<HUB><sub>N</sub>` with a digit that mirrors the Alt+N shortcut. Click chip → navigates to `/app/com/scheduling/<HUB>` in the same tab. `_attachHubShortcuts()` (called from constructor) wires Alt+1..5; `_detachHubShortcuts()` (called from `dispose`) cleans up. Renders nothing when only one hub has been visited (zero clutter on first install).

**Progress-toast propagation** — wired `RouteAssistantToast.progress` into 5 more bulk-sync sites:
- `_runBulkPriceScrape` (single-phase)
- `_runBulkCarrierScrape` (single-phase)
- `_runBulkEnterpriseMetaSync` (single-phase, dynamic todo count)
- `_runRefreshContractualPartners` (single-phase, by enterprise id)
- `_runBulkMarketScrape` (3-phase: families → historic → inventory; phaseLabel changes per phase, single rolling bar across the union)
- `_runBulkDemandSync` (2-phase: historic + inventory)
Each follows the same shape: open `progressHandle` on entry, pump `update(...)` from inside `onProgress`, call `complete(...)` with terminal type (success / warn / error) at the end. Bulk-sync UX is now uniform across all 6 (ORS already done in the previous slice).

**N2 — Notification center.**
- `RouteAssistantToast` now keeps a session-scoped `_history` array (capped at 50) of every toast fired. Each entry: `{id, message, type, timestamp, action: {label, fn} | null}`. The `action.fn` is kept by reference — history is in-memory only, NOT persisted to chrome.storage (functions aren't serialisable). Closing the tab clears it.
- `RouteAssistantToast.getHistory()` / `clearHistory()` accessors.
- New 🔔 panel-header button between settings ⚙ and config ⇅. Click opens a popover anchored under the bell.
- Popover: header (🔔 title · count · Clear button) + scrollable body (reverse-chronological list, each row = colored type-dot · message · "3m ago" · "↻ Undo" hint when re-runnable) + footer hint.
- Click any row with an action → re-fires `action.fn()`. Errors caught + reflected via `RouteAssistantToast.error`. Closing the popover triggers automatic outside-click + Escape teardown.
- New module-scoped helper `_formatRelativeTime(ts)` — "just now" / "12s ago" / "3m ago" / "1h ago" / "2d ago" / localised date for ≥ 7 days.

### Active prompts (Q+, foundation extension) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant settings drawer + floating toasts. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/alert-rules-store.js` (NEW), `modules/route-assistant/alert-evaluator.js` (NEW), extensions to `panel.js` + `toast-host.js`, `manifest.json`.

Turns the panel from passive data into active prompts. Declarative per-row rules — "fire a notification when RM% jumps > 5pp on a starred route" or "alert me when the ORS rating gap drops below 10" — that evaluate on every panel mount against the diff-decorated rows and fire toasts for triggers.

**`RouteAssistantAlertRulesStore`** — persistent rules store at `routeAssistant:alertRules` → `{rules: [<RuleRecord>], updatedAt}`. Each rule:
```js
{
  id, label, field,
  operator:      "increased_by" | "decreased_by" | "above" | "below",
  threshold:     <number>,
  scope:         "watchlist" | "all",
  severity:      "info" | "warn" | "error",
  enabled:       <bool>,
  cooldownHours: <number, default 24>,
  lastFiredByRoute: {<HUB>-<DEST>: <ms epoch>},
  createdAt, updatedAt
}
```
CRUD via `add` / `update` / `remove` / `recordFired` / `load` / `saveAll`. Records normalised on every read so malformed rules don't crash the panel.

**`RouteAssistantAlertEvaluator`** — pure-function `evaluate(rules, rows, ctx)` returning the list of triggered alerts. No side effects, no DOM. Reads `row._diff.<field>` for `increased_by` / `decreased_by` operators, or `row[<field>]` for `above` / `below`. Watchlist scope short-circuits to rows where `row._starred`. Per-rule per-route cooldown suppresses repeats within `rule.cooldownHours`. `availableFields()` exposes the catalog of 11 valid alert targets (score, demand, competition, ORS, RM tightness) to the settings UI dropdown.

**Panel wiring:**
- Constructor: `_alertRules = []` + `_alertFiredThisMount = new Set()`. Fired-set prevents the same rule+route from re-toasting on intra-mount re-renders.
- `refresh()` reloads rules via `_loadAlertRules()` and resets the fired set.
- `_renderRows` calls `_evaluateAndFireAlerts(this.scoredRows)` AFTER `_decorateRowsWithDiffs` and AFTER `_starred` decoration. Each triggered alert fires `RouteAssistantToast.show(message, {type, duration: 8000, action: {label: "View", fn: () => _scrollToRouteRow(dest)}})` and persists `recordFired(ruleId, routeKey)` for cross-mount cooldown.
- `_scrollToRouteRow(destIata)` finds the matching row in the current table, smooth-scrolls into view, flashes amber background for 2s.

**Settings UI (`_renderAlertRulesSection`)** — amber-tinted expander mounted below the ORS Sandbox section. Lists existing rules with enable toggle + severity dot + scope chip + cooldown chip + delete (×, undoable via `_undoableSave`). Add-rule row: field dropdown (from `availableFields()`) + operator dropdown + threshold input + scope dropdown + severity dropdown + Add button.

### N1 progress toasts — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** floating toasts during long-running bulk syncs. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/toast-host.js` (extended), `panel.js`.

Extends the toast primitive with three new methods:
- `RouteAssistantToast.progress(message, opts)` — creates a STICKY toast (no auto-dismiss) with an inline progress bar. Returns `{id, dismiss(), update(patch), complete(patch)}`.
- `RouteAssistantToast.update(id, {message?, progressPct?, progressLabel?})` — updates the message + bar fill + secondary label in place.
- `RouteAssistantToast.complete(id, {type?, message?, duration?})` — converts the sticky toast to auto-dismissing with terminal type (success / warn / error). Bar fills to 100%, restyles, auto-dismisses after `duration` (default 3.5s).

**Wired into `_runBulkOrsScrape`** as the canonical example. The existing `onProgress` callback now also pumps `progressHandle.update(...)` with `progressPct = 100 × done / total` and `progressLabel = "12 / 87 routes"`. On completion: `progressHandle.complete({type: "success" | "warn" | "error", message: ...})`. User can tab away from the panel during a 5+min scrape and watch the bottom-right corner instead of the inline status line.

**Future wirings (~5 LOC each):** `_runBulkMarketScrape`, `_runBulkDemandSync`, `_runBulkCarrierScrape`, `_runBulkEnterpriseMetaSync`, `_runRefreshContractualPartners`. Each already has an `onProgress` callback — adding the progress toast handle is mechanical.

### Q11 single-step undo (foundation extension) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel save dialogs (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`.

A panel-level helper `_undoableSave({label, perform, restore, afterRestore, type, durationMs, actionLabel})` that wraps any save call. Caller captures the previous state first, hands the helper a `perform` closure that runs the save and a `restore` closure that re-applies the captured state. On success, the helper fires a `RouteAssistantToast.show(...)` with an Undo button wired to `restore`. Default duration 6000ms (longer than usual to give time to react). On failure the helper fires an error toast and re-throws so the caller can keep its own error path.

**Wired into 4 sites this session:**
- Profit-modifier popover Save → "Override saved for JFK→LAX [Undo]"
- Profit-modifier popover Clear → "Override cleared for JFK→LAX [Undo]"
- Route-note popover Save → "Note saved for JFK→LAX [Undo]"
- Route-note popover Delete → "Note deleted for JFK→LAX [Undo]"

Each site captures the prev `row.override` / `routeNoteMap.get(pairKey)` record into a closure, runs the save, then exposes a restore that handles both the "had a previous record → re-save it" and the "previously empty → remove the new save" paths. After Undo: a 2.5s "Reverted" info toast.

**Future call sites** (~15 minutes each to wire): full override editor (line ~8964), service-config popover Save / Clear (line ~8809 / 8740), batch override editor (line ~5100), settings export/import Apply, ORS Sandbox Calibrate-T (already has banner — could add Undo for the per-route T overwrite).

**Why it's foundation:** every store-save call site now has a stock pattern for "make this reversible." Future stores (alert thresholds, saved table views, strategy presets, etc.) will adopt it for free. Cumulative QoL — the user's anxiety-cost of clicking Save drops to near-zero because the worst-case is one click of Undo away.

### Foundation QoL primitives — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel (`/app/com/scheduling*`). **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/toast-host.js` (NEW, ~167 LOC), extensions to `modules/route-assistant/panel.js`, `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `css/content.css`, `manifest.json`.

A small foundation slice — primitives that future features can hook into, not iteration on existing ones:

- **U14 — Toast notifications.** New `RouteAssistantToast` global with `show(message, opts)` / `dismiss(id)` / `clearAll()` / `info|success|warn|error` shortcuts. Singleton DOM container at the bottom-right of the viewport, slide-in animation, auto-dismiss timers (default 3.5s; pass `0` for persistent), optional action button (e.g. Undo). Stack capped at 6; oldest dismissed when exceeded. Re-using an `id` replaces the existing toast in place (good for spammed save calls).
  - **Distinct from the existing `_noteToast`** — `_noteToast` is a single inline status line under the panel header (still in use at 17 sites). `RouteAssistantToast` is for *floating transient* notifications with optional one-click actions. Both kept; future features pick the right one for the situation.
  - **Wired into 3 sites this slice (validation, not exhaustive):**
    - Watchlist toggle — toast with one-click Undo. ⭐ Starred / Unstarred routes get a 3.5s confirmation that lets the user reverse without the round trip back to the destination cell.
    - Route note save / delete — success toast confirming the write took.
    - ORS Sandbox Calibrate-T — success toast mirrors the in-card banner so the user sees the result even if they're scrolled away from the sandbox card.
  - **Future features that will reuse the primitive:** Q11 single-step undo (every store-save call site), bulk-sync completion notifications, alert thresholds (Q+), import-config success / failure, calibrate-flagged batch result.

- **Q12 — Persistent free-text search.** New 🔍 input on the controls bar — first control so it's the most discoverable. Filters table rows by IATA / city-name / route-note-text (case-insensitive substring). Persists to `settings.routeAssistant.searchQuery` with a 200ms debounce (typing stays fluid; one storage write + one re-render per pause). Native `<input type="search">` element so the X clear-button is free. Composes with existing filters (status / minScore / fleetFlyableOnly / search query all AND together).

- **U15 — Group-header hover highlight.** Every `<th>` and `<td>` in the route table now carries a `data-group="<group>"` attribute. Mouseenter on a group header (the row above the column headers) toggles a CSS class `aes-grp-hover` on every cell with the matching `data-group`, lifting the column-band's brightness via inset box-shadow. CSS rule lives in `css/content.css` (only addition this slice). Pure CSS would have needed `:has()` + universal sibling combinators that don't quite exist; JS toggle is ~15 LOC, trivial.

- **U1 — Sticky table header.** Already shipped in an earlier session — verified during this work. The thead's `position: sticky` plus the per-column `top: 22px` for the column-header row + `top: 0` for the group-header row keep both rows glued during vertical scroll. Frozen leading columns (Score + Dest) via `position: sticky; left: …px` were also already in place. **Removed from the QoL backlog as already-done; documented here so a future session doesn't re-implement.**

**Why these four together:** they're foundation primitives, not iterations on the just-shipped ORS Sandbox. The toast system is the bigger one — every future feature that writes state can call `RouteAssistantToast.show(...)` instead of inventing its own confirmation UI. The search bar compounds with every existing filter. Group-hover is a cheap discoverability win. Slice cost: ~280 LOC.

### ORS Sandbox (Letter I — slice 1) — shipped this session
**Surfaces in:** Route Assistant panel header (`/app/com/scheduling*`) — new 🧪 toggle next to 📊 Wave View. **Files:** `modules/route-assistant/ors-model.js` (NEW, ~510 LOC), extensions to `modules/route-assistant/panel.js` (~1380 LOC), `modules/route-assistant/settings-store.js`, `manifest.json`.

A new panel mode that replaces the table with a per-route pricing simulator. Pick a route → drag price / frequency / comfort sliders → projected rank, market share, pax/wk, revenue/wk, profit/wk update live. Read-only against AS — no writes.

**Pipeline (closed-form, transparent, every constant settings-tunable):**
1. **Rating projection (per cabin class).** `newRating = clamp(baseRating − α_price × (newPriceY − observedPriceY)/observedPriceY + α_comfort × comfortDelta, [0.5×base, 1.5×base])`. Linear-in-percent (NOT log-ratio) — defensible 0–30% deviation, surfaces a clamp note outside that. Defaults `α_price = 8` rating points per ±100% price change, `α_comfort = 5` per service-level step. Mutates the rating only on "every-leg-ours" connections; mixed-ownership multi-leg connections (interline) keep their fixed rating + a `mixedOwnership` note.
2. **Rank projection.** Re-sort the cached `byClass.<class>.connections[]` (top 50) by mutated rating descending; mirror `RouteAssistantOrsScraper.computeRanks` (`ors-scraper.js:486–528`) to derive `rankAny / rankFirstLegOurs / rankAllOurs / rankNonstop / rankBookable`.
