(1) `_runBulkMarketScrape` now folds in `bulkScrapeHistoric` + inventory `bulkScrape` after the markets families finish; one click in the Market Analysis expander populates everything the panel needs. Status text cycles through phases. (2) After the folded pass, `_applyCachedDemand` runs automatically — demand columns refresh without a separate Demand Depth sync. (3) Defaults flipped: `useRealDemandForLF: true` (graceful paxScore fallback when inputs missing), `classCoverage: "full"` (5 payloads — needed for I anyway), `historicMaxAgeDays: 14`. Existing users keep persisted prefs via deep-merge. (4) New `marketAnalysis.autoRefreshOnMount` (default true) + `staleThresholdDays` (14): on mount, when `lastBulkScrapeAt` is null/stale, the panel surfaces a banner and kicks off `_runBulkMarketScrape()` after a 5s grace period; user can hit Skip to suppress for `staleThresholdDays`. (5) Demand-depth column tooltips now combine the static preamble with row state — "No data" + `derivationNotes` + a CTA when missing; "Last derived: X day(s) ago" with STALE flag when present. Stale rows render in `#9ca3af` so the user notices. New `RouteAssistantPanel._demandTooltip(field, row)` + `_demandIsStale(row)` statics; `_syncRenderContext` mirrors `staleThresholdDays`. The standalone Demand Depth sync stays as a granular fallback. |
| ~~**J slice 3**~~ | ~~Used Aircraft Scanner — surface deal-metric tooltips with input breakdown~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** `daysToBreakEvenBreakdown()` and `seatKmYearCostBreakdown()` already retained intermediates and `decorate()` attached them to the row; results-table.js gained `_formatPricePerSeatTooltip`, `_formatMaintTooltip`, `_formatFleetTooltip` to fill remaining gaps so every deal column (`$/seat`, `$/seat·km/yr`, `BE (days)`, `Maint.`, `Fleet`, `Route-fit`) now shows a multi-line breakdown on hover. Inner-pill `title` attributes removed from `_renderMaintCell` / `_renderFleetCell` so the long tooltip wins. |
| ~~**J slice 4**~~ | ~~Used Aircraft Scanner — route-fit upgrade beyond range~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session as v3.** v2 had per-flight demand check (seats×LF ≥ paxScore×scale). v3 adds the weekly-frequency gate using the `weeklyFlights` field already on `topRoutes`: a regional that fits one flight's demand can still fail a route flown 14×/wk. New `frequencyLimited` counter, label format `x / y  (−A demand, −B freq)`, tunable via `routeFit.weeklyDemandPerScorePoint` (default 100, paxScore=10 → 1000 weekly seats). Routes without `weeklyFlights` skip the gate (no penalty — Tier 1 hasn't seen them yet). Tooltip extended to surface all three sequential gates. |
| ~~**F slice 2**~~ | ~~Carriers — AS-native enterprise enrichment in the Cmp tooltip~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** New `modules/route-assistant/enterprise-meta-scraper.js` fetches `/app/info/enterprises/<id>` per AS competitor and caches `{name, iata, bannerUrl, avatarUrl}` at `routeAssistant:enterpriseMeta:<id>` (90d TTL, single global key per id — non-directional). Cmp pill replaced its native `title=""` with a custom DOM popover (hover-open with 200ms delay, 250ms grace on leave, click-to-pin, Escape/outside-click dismiss — same pattern as `_openProfitModifierPopover`). Each row in the popover shows avatar + clickable name link to `/app/info/enterprises/<id>` + banner + share% + ▲/▼ change + rank, sorted by share. Falls back to the plain-text tooltip when `marketSharePax` is empty (routes without markets-scraper hits). New "Sync enterprise data" CTA in the Carriers expander batches the per-id fetches (default 4 concurrent / 600ms stagger). |
| ~~**F slice 3**~~ | ~~Carriers — interlining (IL) agreement indicator~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** Source resolved to `/app/info/enterprises/<your_id>?tab=1` ("Contractual partners" tab) — NOT `/action/enterprise/interlining` as the original guess suggested. New `modules/route-assistant/contractual-partners-scraper.js` mirrors `enterprise-meta-scraper.js`. Cache `routeAssistant:contractualPartners:<enterpriseId>` (non-directional, keyed by *your-own* enterprise id, NOT competitor). `_buildCarrierRow` reads `_partnersByEnterpriseId` and renders `⇄` (green) for INTERLINING and optionally `✦` (purple) for ALLIANCE next to the partner's name. New "Contractual partners" sub-panel in the Carriers expander with own-enterprise-IDs input + Refresh CTA + glyph toggles. Settings: `myEnterpriseIds`, `partnersMaxAgeDays` (30d default), `lastPartnersSyncAt`, `showInterliningGlyph`, `showAllianceGlyph`. Captures every relation type verbatim (LESSOR / ALLIANCE / INTERLINING / future tokens) so future relation types still surface. |

### ORS Sandbox roadmap (slices 2–6)

The ORS Sandbox (Letter I) ships precision + trust + workflow value across five slices. Slice 1 + 1.5 are done; this section is the path forward. Each slice maps to one of the user's three goals: **precise** (slices 2 + 3 — model correctness + trust), **obviously effective** (slice 4 + 5 — visible workflow value), **efficient** (slice 6 — UX/perf polish). Slice 2 unblocks Tier 3 Auto-Pricing write-back; slice 3 makes Tier 3 safe to ship.

**Why this ordering:** precision before workflow. A sweet-spot finder (4a) that runs against an inaccurate model (slice 2 not yet done) gives confidently-wrong answers and burns user trust. Slice 3's back-test logging needs slice 2's improved projections to compare against — measuring the wrong model isn't useful. Slice 5 visualisations get their data from the same scan helper slice 4a introduces, so 5 follows 4. Slice 6 is pure polish — last.

#### Slice 2 — Model precision (Tier 1)

The biggest correctness gaps. Four of five shipped this session; **2c** is the only remaining slice 2 item.

| # | What | Files | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| ~~**2a**~~ | ~~Frequency lever with synthesised own-connections~~ | ~~`ors-model.js`~~ | ✅ Shipped — `_synthesizeOwnConnections` clones an own + nonstop template; cap = +ceil(0.5×currentFreq) ∧ MAX_FOR_SOFTMAX − existing. **No timestamps needed** — connection schemas in cache have none and rank/softmax don't read them. Frequency-down keeps slice-1 LF dilute. |
| ~~**2b**~~ | ~~Independent per-class price control~~ | ~~`panel.js`, `ors-model.js`, `settings-store.js`~~ | ✅ Shipped — scenario shape `priceMultipliers: {Y, C, F}` with one-shot legacy migration; UI collapses to a single slider when only Y is cached; pax-pool elasticity reads Y multiplier only (pool is Y-anchored per demand-derivator). |
| **2c** | Per-route price elasticity | `ors-model.js:project` (`elast` block), `demand-derivator.js` extension | **Deferred — needs accumulated data.** `route.paxElasticity` is already per-route; `params.ratingPriceElasticity` (the rating-shift constant) is global. Add `params.ratingPriceElasticityByRoute` derived from a new regression in `demand-derivator.js` that emits `ratingPriceElasticity` alongside `paxElasticity`. Per-route override via the existing route-overrides-store. |
| ~~**2d**~~ | ~~Cargo branch~~ | ~~`ors-model.js`, `panel.js`~~ | ✅ Shipped — `_projectCargo` mirror, cargo slider scales `cargoYieldPerKgKm` via `override.cargoYieldPerKgKm` hook in profit-estimator (no estimator changes). Cargo demand pool shifts via `cargoElasticity` when `cargoMultiplier !== 1`. Output adds `cargoPerWeek` to baseline/projected/delta. |
| ~~**2e**~~ | ~~Visual rating-clamp annotation~~ | ~~`panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxResultsCard`~~ | ✅ Shipped — `renderRow` accepts an annotation; `_clampedClasses(result)` reads `perClass.<cls>.priceRatio` (≥±0.5 threshold matches `RATING_CLAMP_LOW/HIGH`); projected Rating cell shows amber `*` + tooltip listing clamped classes. |

#### Slice 3 — Trust + audit (Tier 1, after slice 2)

Slice 2 makes projections better; slice 3 makes the user trust them. Without this slice, all projections look equally trustworthy and the user has no signal that calibration is working over time. ~400 LOC.

| # | What | Files | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| ~~**3a**~~ | ~~Confidence indicators on the projection card~~ | ~~`panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxResultsCard`~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** Confidence pill above Outcome table aggregates T-calibration freshness + ORS data age + connection-count sample size into high/medium/low. See §1 "ORS Sandbox slice 3". |
| ~~**3b**~~ | ~~Back-test logging~~ | ~~new `modules/route-assistant/sandbox-backtest-store.js`~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** New per-route directional store at `routeAssistant:sandboxBacktest:<HUB>-<DEST>`; calibrate-T hook logs immediately, bulk markets scrape back-fills `observed.share` within an 8-day window. Tier 3 apply hook pre-staged via `trigger: "tier3-apply"`. See §1 + §4. |
| ~~**3c**~~ | ~~Model-fit summary~~ | ~~`panel.js:_renderOrsSandboxSection`~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session.** New "Model fit: bias / RMSE" row in the ORS Sandbox settings expander; `_computeOrsSandboxModelFit(allBacktests)` aggregates routes with ≥3 back-filled observations; Refresh button reloads. See §1 "ORS Sandbox slice 3". |

#### Slice 4 — Workflow ergonomics (Tier 2, multi-session)

Visible workflow value. Each is a self-contained sub-feature. ~700 LOC across the four.

| # | What | Files | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| **4a** | Sweet-spot finder | `ors-model.js:scanPriceCurve` (new pure helper), `panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxScenarioCard` (button + result preview) | "Find optimal price" button — scan `priceMultiplier` from 0.7 to 1.3 in 5% steps, return the profit-maximising point. One click, no manual sliding. Result line: "Optimal: 0.92× → +12% profit/wk" that the user can click to apply. |
| **4b** | A/B scenario comparison | `panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxResultsCard` extension | Pin a scenario (📌 button), run another, render two projected columns side-by-side with their own delta-vs-baseline. State on `this._orsSandboxPinnedResult`; clearable via a clear-pin button. |
| **4c** | Saved named scenarios per route | new `modules/route-assistant/sandbox-scenarios-store.js` | Persist a scenario as `{routeKey, name, scenario}` under `routeAssistant:sandboxScenarios:<HUB>-<DEST>` (directional, mirrors `route-overrides-store`). Quick-toggle dropdown above the sliders. Cap at 5 per route. |
| **4d** | Multi-route batch projection | new `panel.js:_runSandboxBatch` + `_buildSandboxBatchCard` | Apply the current scenario to top-N visible routes simultaneously. Roll up projected vs baseline profit across all routes. Answers "what if I cut all top-20 fares 5%?" — currently unanswerable. |

#### Slice 5 — Visualisations (Tier 2)

Optional but highest "obviously effective" payoff. ~500 LOC.

| # | What | Files | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| **5a** | Price-vs-profit sparkline | `panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxResultsCard` (new sparkline cell), `ors-model.js:scanPriceCurve` (re-used) | Inline sparkline of profit/wk across `priceMultiplier ∈ [0.7, 1.3]`. Sweet spot visible at a glance. Reuses slice 4a scan helper. |
| **5b** | Historical pax/wk overlay | `panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxResultsCard`, reads `routeAssistant:lastSnapshot:<HUB>` | Tiny 8-week trend line under the projected pax/wk number. Anchors "current" in context. Data is already cached. |
| **5c** | Sensitivity sweep heatmap | new card builder | 5×5 grid: `priceMultiplier` × `frequency`. Cell colour-coded by projected profit/wk delta. Click a cell to apply. Heavier render but answers "where in price-freq space am I?" instantly. |

#### Slice 6 — Efficiency polish (Tier 3, last)

UX/perf only — no model changes. ~200 LOC.

| # | What | Files | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| **6a** | Memoize `_assembleOrsSandboxRoute` | `panel.js:_assembleOrsSandboxRoute` | Currently rebuilds the entire route bundle on every recompute. Memoize on `(routeKey, lastFetchAt-of-each-cache)` — if underlying caches haven't changed, return the cached bundle. 5–10× speedup on slow projections. |
| **6b** | Computing indicator | `panel.js:_recomputeOrsSandbox` | If `project()` takes >100ms (rare but possible on routes with 50+ connections), show a faint "computing…" overlay on the results card. Today the card freezes briefly. |
| **6c** | Toast batching during bulk calibrate | `panel.js:_calibrateOrsSandboxT` + future "calibrate all" button | If multiple calibrate calls fire within 2s, aggregate into one toast: "Calibrated T for 8 routes". |
| ~~**6d**~~ | ~~Reset/undo per slider~~ | ~~`panel.js:_buildOrsSandboxScenarioCard`~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session alongside slice 3.** ↺ button on each Y/C/F price slider + cargo slider + frequency input + comfort selector. Restores baseline + dispatches the control's native input/change event so the existing onChange recompute fires. See §1 "ORS Sandbox slice 3 + slice 6d". |

#### Cross-cutting dependencies

- **Tier 3 (Auto-Pricing write-back)** is gated on slice 2 (better projections) and slice 3b (back-test data to validate against). The Tier 3 row in the §9 "Next" table above is unblocked once 2+3b ship.
- **Slice 4c (saved scenarios)** is independent; could ship before slices 2/3 if user prefers a workflow-first taste. Slice 4a/b/d are cleaner after 2 because they all compound on projection accuracy.
- **Slice 6** can ship at any time — pure polish, no dependencies.

### Daily-driver QoL backlog (user-requested enumeration, end of K session)

The user asked "what else would be useful and practical?" after K shipped. The shortlist below is cheap-to-ship, high-frequency-use upgrades that would compound with everything currently in the panel. **Next slice picked: Diff against last visit.**

| Slice | What | Why it's practical | Est |
|---|---|---|---|
| ~~**Diff against last visit**~~ | ✅ **Shipped earlier this session.** New cache `routeAssistant:lastSnapshot:<HUB>` → `{hub, server, scrapedAt, rows: [{destIata, score, paxScore, cargoScore, weeklyFlights, airlineCount, competitorCount, paxDemandPool, cargoDemandPool, ourPaxShare, orsRatingGapToTop, rmTightness}]}` (row field `orsRatingGapToTop` rather than the spec's `ratingGapToTop` so the diff key matches the row property the cell reads). Baseline loaded once per (mount, hub) pair in `refresh()` into `this._diffPrevSnapshot`; rows decorated with `_diff.<field>` scalars in `_renderRows` before sort/draw; new snapshot written at end of `_drawTable` from `this.scoredRows` (overwrites storage but the in-memory baseline survives the mount, so intra-mount re-renders keep showing the same deltas). 11 numeric column render closures append a small grey ▲/▼ badge via `_appendDiffBadge`. Cmp column uses array-fallback (`["competitorCount", "airlineCount"]`) to track whichever source produced the cell value. | Turns the panel from a snapshot-stare into a change-detector — "on this hub overnight: Cmp +2 on JFK→LAX, RM% +5pp on JFK→ORD, ORS gap dropped on three routes." Replaces eye-scanning the table for what's changed. | ~175 LOC |
| ~~**Watchlist / starred routes**~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session as Daily-driver QoL slice 2.** See §1. The `RouteAssistantWatchlistStore` already existed from a prior session (`modules/route-assistant/watchlist-store.js`) but was unwired — manifest registration + panel.js wiring + `RA_WATCH_TRIGGERS` evaluation + the destIata cell `_buildWatchlistStar` glyph + `_sortRows` float-to-top + `settings.routeAssistant.watchlist.{floatStarredToTop, showAlertBadges}` finished it. Filters still apply to starred rows in v1; cross-tab `chrome.storage.onChanged` listener for the watchlist key was deferred. The "separate Watched table above the main one" optional sub-feature was deferred — float-to-top + visible star is enough to surface them without splitting the layout. | Keeps the few routes you're actively managing front-and-centre instead of having to re-find them in the score-sorted list every visit. | ~120 LOC |
| ~~**Route notes**~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session as Daily-driver QoL slice 4.** New `modules/route-assistant/route-note-store.js` (directional pair-key cache, mirrors `route-overrides-store.js`). 📝 icon added to the Dest cell's existing icon row (📅 📦 🛫 📝); always rendered (subdued grey when empty, full color when present) so adding a note is discoverable for empty routes — small deviation from the spec's "when present" wording. Click opens a popover with textarea + char counter (max 500) + Save / Delete / Cancel. Cell-wide tooltip surfaces the note text on hover; the icon's title shows the first 80 chars. Storage cache `routeAssistant:routeNote:<HUB>-<DEST>` (directional, empty saves remove the key). Popover lifecycle mirrors `_openProfitModifierPopover` (outside-click + Escape + viewport-clamped position). Save mutates row + map in place + `_renderRows` so the icon flips color without a full refresh. | Memory across long-running games — "tried 2x daily, dropped — too much C-class capacity"; "watching for AA entry"; "served by partner under interline". | ~225 LOC |
| ~~**Settings export / import**~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session as Daily-driver QoL slice 3.** New "⇅" button in the panel header opens a small popup menu with two items. Export downloads `aes-config-<server>-<date>.json` containing both `routeAssistant` and `usedAircraftScanner` blobs in a `{format: "aes-config", version, exportedAt, server, ...}` envelope. Import reads a JSON file, validates `format === "aes-config"`, recursively diffs against current settings, and shows a modal listing every added/removed/changed leaf path with from→to values; Apply writes both blobs back via their respective `save()` calls (deep-merge handles any missing keys against current defaults). Cancel/Escape/click-outside dismisses the modal. See §1 + §10 for the envelope contract. | Migrate between worlds, share configs with friends, back up tuning that took weeks. Single-click recovery if a sync goes wrong. | ~60 LOC |
| **Compare two enterprises** | Click any two enterprise pills in the Cmp popover → side-by-side diff modal of every overlap route showing each side's share + rank + change. | Useful for benchmarking specific competitors (who's eating your share?) without manually walking the leaderboard. Reuses F slice 2 popover infra. | ~180 LOC |
| ~~**Alert thresholds**~~ | ✅ **Shipped this session as "Active prompts (Q+)".** New `RouteAssistantAlertRulesStore` + pure-function `RouteAssistantAlertEvaluator` + amber settings expander. Declarative rules with operators (`increased_by` / `decreased_by` / `above` / `below`), watchlist-vs-all scope, per-rule per-route cooldown (default 24h). Triggers fire `RouteAssistantToast.warn` with a "View" action that scrolls/highlights the row. See §1 "Active prompts (Q+)". **Defer to slice 2:** desktop notifications via `chrome.notifications` (Q16, separate permission), trigger on N-day persistence (e.g. "RM% > 0.95 for 7 days running"), per-rule notification channel routing. | — | — |
| ~~**Cross-server view**~~ | **Subsumed by Letter L canopy MVP — see "Letter L (canopy) — multi-account MVP plan" sub-section below.** Slice L5 (cross-account dashboard) is the unified read-only aggregator across worlds and airlines; Slices L1–L3 do the storage refactor that makes it possible. Originally scoped at ~300 LOC for a Used-Aircraft-Scanner-dashboard tile but the storage scoping work is the same as for L, so they merge. | — | — |
| **L** (user-requested, ultimate goal — **v2 plan committed 2026-04-27**) | Multi-account federation — register, scope, federate, and coordinate across multiple AS accounts | **v2 plan now exists** (passive observation, no auto-login, local-only — but federation-rich: affiliation graph, Strategy DNA, combined-supply analytics, cross-account wave coordination) — see the "Letter L (canopy) — multi-account federation plan (v2)" sub-section below for the full 5-layer design + 13-slice phased program + storage scoping rubric + new §10 invariants. Detailed plan at `.claude/plans/zany-hopping-clover.md`. **Canopy auto-login** parts (encrypted credential vault, hidden-tab orchestrator, login automation) are explicitly deferred to "Letter L+" pending a re-evaluation of AS ToS and CAPTCHA/MFA fragility. **Original full vision (deferred auto-login parts only — analytics + scheduling pieces are now in-scope as L4–L12):** the user runs several AS accounts/enterprises (today: FLY NYON. + NYON., potentially more across game worlds). Today each requires a separate browser login + manual context switch. Goal is a single AES interface that holds saved credentials + session cookies for every account, transparently logs in/out as the user navigates, and aggregates every account's routes / fleet / yields / market shares / ORS rank into one consolidated dashboard. The user manages a whole "canopy" of routes across all profiles from one panel and never thinks about which account they're logged into. **Sub-features (auto-login pieces deferred):** (1) Account-vault store with encrypted credentials + per-account session cookies, refreshed on demand. (2) Background tab orchestrator that opens a hidden tab per account, logs in if expired, runs a scrape pass, closes the tab. (3) Aggregator layer above existing per-server caches — new schema like `aesCanopy:account:<accountId>` referencing existing `<server>` keys. (4) Cross-account columns in the Route Assistant (e.g. "Best account for this route", "Routes my OTHER airline already flies"). (5) Cross-account scheduler that flags conflicts (same hub-pair operated by multiple of your airlines = pricing self-cannibalisation). (6) Permission/safety gates — bulk write actions (Tier 3 pricing apply, station opens) confirm per account. **Risks:** AS may have ToS limits around session sharing / multi-instance use; needs to read AS rules on automation. Login automation is fragile (CAPTCHA, MFA). Heavy storage growth. Probably the largest single feature on the roadmap — Tier 4, multi-session build, ~2000+ LOC. **Prereqs:** essentially every existing feature (this depends on each scraper being canopy-aware; refactor work first — that refactor is what L1–L3 deliver). |

### Letter L (canopy) — multi-account federation plan (v2)

**Status:** v2 plan committed; no code shipped. Promoted from the Daily-driver QoL backlog row (above) to a dedicated sub-section. Subsumes the "Cross-server view" backlog row entirely. Detailed plan at `.claude/plans/zany-hopping-clover.md`. v2 expanded scope from "registry plumbing + read-only dashboard" (v1) to "registry + affiliation graph + Strategy DNA + combined-supply analytics + cross-account wave coordination."

**User decisions captured this session (2026-04-27):**
- Scope: every `(server, airline)` tuple is a registered account — fully cross-server + cross-airline.
- Sync mechanism: **passive observation only** — register on visit, no credential vault, no auto-login, no background tabs. Auto-login canopy (the original Letter L brainstorm) is explicitly **deferred** to a future "Letter L+" slice; the plan leaves all hooks needed to drop it in without re-keying data.
- Cloud sync between devices: deferred (local-only).
- **DNA scope: hybrid** — global template `aesCanopy:dna` + sparse per-account override `aesCanopy:dnaOverride:acct:<id>`; `effectiveDna(accountId) = deepMerge(template, override)` at leaf level; drift always computed against effective.
- **Affiliation precedence: user override always wins** — `userOverride: true` flag is absolute; auto-classification only fills empty slots; "Reset to detected" clears the flag.
- **Wave coordination: full editor with cross-account schedule writes** (Slices L9–L12) — Tier-3 two-gate apply pattern non-negotiable; per-account confirm; cross-account audit log + 60s undo window.
- **No kin hierarchy** — all self-tagged accounts are equal in the affiliation graph; no `parentEnterpriseId` field. AS's parent/subsidiary distinction is not modeled at the data level.

**Five conceptual layers (above the v1 storage plumbing):**

1. **Affiliation graph** — every observed enterprise classified as `self / allied / interline / codeshare / neutral / adversary`. Auto-feed from `routeAssistant:contractualPartners:<myEnterpriseId>` (existing F slice 3 cache). User override always wins. Storage: single global blob `aesCanopy:affiliations`. Joins everything downstream.
2. **Strategy DNA** — persistent playstyle profile (service-mix, network-shape, cargo emphasis, manufacturer prefs, size-mix targets, risk profile, country focus, tempo, brand stance, growth posture). Hybrid scope (template + per-account override). Drives DNA-fit scoring per account + per opportunity, default settings template for new accounts, and drift detection.
3. **Combined-supply / effective-competition** — RA panel "Canopy view" toggle adds columns: MyWk (combined kin weekly flights), Cmp* (true effective competitor count), FShare (combined kin marketShare), Cannib (self-cannibalization warning), Gap? (high-demand kin-absent route). Pure-function aggregator over `(routes, accounts, affiliations)`.
4. **Geographic awareness** — country / region as first-class organizing dimensions. Per-country demand pool, per-country kin presence, cross-country flow matrix, regulatory lens (domestic / continental / intercontinental). New "Geography" tab on the canopy dashboard. Region groupings stored at `aesCanopy:regions`.
5. **Cross-account wave coordination** — interline gateway detection, per-gateway wave audit (MCT-driven connection feasibility), wave alignment recommendations (Pareto-frontier shifts), canopy wave editor (multi-row Gantt with drag-shift + live connection counts), slot model (`aesCanopy:slots:<airport>`), interline-aware schedule preset solver (canopy weight in `schedule-builder`). Schedule write-back via Tier-3 two-gate apply mirroring `pricing-applier`.

**Why this matters:** today the extension is single-account-implicit. A user running multiple AirlineSim airlines (FLY NYON + NYON., potentially across worlds) gets cross-airline data corruption on shared hubs (one airline's `routeAssistant:override:JFK-LAX` clobbers the other's; ORS-rank readings overwrite each other since `isOurs` differs by airline; `routeAssistant:topRoutes:<HUB>` and `lastSnapshot:<HUB>` collide), no unified view across airlines, and settings tuned for one airline silently apply to the other. v2 goes further: the canopy doesn't just isolate accounts, it **federates** them — the user's airlines should reinforce each other (combined supply, gap-detection, wave coordination, cross-account interlining), not operate as parallel silos.

**Storage scoping rubric (single most important design decision):**

| Class | Examples | Scoping |
|---|---|---|
| **A · World-fact** (airline-agnostic, expensive to scrape, shareable) | `routeAssistant:demand:<IATA>`, `routeAssistant:distance:*`, `routeAssistant:typeSpec:<typeId>`, `routeAssistant:fuelPriceIndex`, `routeAssistant:fuelBurnOverride:<typeId>`, `flightsFrom:<IATA>` | **Stay global.** Every account benefits from another's scrape. |
| **B · Account-perspective observations** (depend on "ours") | `routeAssistant:ors:*`, `routeAssistant:markets:*` (4 families), `routeAssistant:ticketPrice:*`, `routeAssistant:carriers:*`, `routeAssistant:inventory:*`, `routeAssistant:yieldHistory:*`, `routeAssistant:statusHistory:*`, `routeAssistant:lastSnapshot:<HUB>`, `routeAssistant:topRoutes` + `routeAssistant:topRoutes:<HUB>`, `routeAssistant:sandboxBacktest:*`, `routeAssistant:ratingObservations:*`, `routeAssistant:pricingApplyLog` (+ per-route ring), `routeAssistant:enterpriseMeta:*`, `routeAssistant:contractualPartners:*` | **Per-account.** |
| **C · Account-private state** | `routeAssistant:override:*` (full-replace — clash is data-loss), `routeAssistant:routeNote:*`, `routeAssistant:watchlist`, `routeAssistant:alertRules`, `routeAssistant:ratingAlpha:*`, `aircraftFlightPlan:auditLog`, `aircraftFlightPlan:autoApplyLog`, `aircraftFlightPlan:fnApplyLog` | **Per-account.** |
| **D · Account-private settings** | `settings.routeAssistant`, `settings.aircraftFlightPlan`, `settings.usedAircraftScanner` | **Per-account, with a `settings:default:*` template each new account inherits.** |
| **E · Already correctly scoped — no change** | `<server><airline>aircraftFleet`, `<server><airline>schedule`, `aircraftFlightPlan:state:<server>:<aircraftId>`, `<server>aircraftFlights<aircraftId>`, `<server>marketScan:*`, `<server><airline>stationAutomationRun:*`, `aircraftFlightPlan:auditLog:aircraft:<server>:<aircraftId>` | None. |

**Naming convention:** `<existing-prefix>:acct:<accountId>:<existing-suffix>` (e.g., `routeAssistant:override:acct:abc123:JFK-LAX`). Additive — doesn't disturb existing keys; legacy keys stay live as fallback during migration. `<accountId>` = `sha1(server + ":" + airlineCode).slice(0,12)`.

| Phase | # | Title | Scope summary | Est. LOC |
|---|---|---|---|---|

No store refactors yet. | ~400 |
| | **L2** | Class C+D refactor (private state + settings) | `RouteAssistantSettings`, `AesAfpSettings`, `RouteOverridesStore` (full-replace — accountId MUST round-trip through every caller incl. Q2 bulk + Undo restore), `routeNoteStore`, `watchlistStore`, `alertRulesStore`, `ratingAlphaStore`, `statusHistoryStore`, `recentHubs`, `savedViews`. Migration shim. **Highest data-integrity stakes.** | ~900 |
| | **L3** | Class B refactor (account-perspective observations) | ORS, markets (4 families), ticketPrice, carriers, inventory, yieldHistory, sandboxBacktest, ratingObservations, pricingApplyLog (+ per-route ring), `topRoutes` writer (`_publishTopRoutes`), `lastSnapshot` writer (`_writeDiffSnapshot`), enterpriseMeta, contractualPartners, `routeAssistant:yieldBaselines`. | ~700 |

| | **L8** | Fleet balance + DNA-aware acquisitions | UAS gains DNA-fit overlay on candidate cards (uses `AS_TYPE_TO_FAMILY` for manufacturer dim); per-account deployment-fit ("for which account?" picker); cross-account fleet balance dashboard tab (current type/age/seat mix vs effective DNA target). | ~600 |
| **4 Cross-account scheduling** | **L9** | Interline Gateway detector + audit | Walk every kin's schedule + partners' observed schedules; gateways = airports with ≥2 my-side ops; per-gateway wave audit emits realized/potential connection counts under MCT (60 min same-op, 90 min cross-op kin, 120 min interline). Read-only dashboard tab. Audit results cached at `aesCanopy:gateway:<airport>`. | ~700 |
